# Experiment Log

Append-only record of things we tried — probes, hypothesis tests, approaches, benchmarks — whether they worked or not. **Write the entry in the same session the result is known.** Failed and inconclusive experiments matter most: they are what stops future-us from repeating them.

Entry format:

```
### YYYY-MM-DD — short title
- **Question / hypothesis:** what we wanted to know
- **Method:** exact command, or path under scripts/experiments/, or inline snippet
- **Result:** numbers, statuses, exact error text
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** what we now believe and what not to try again
- **Links:** issue #, commit, related files
```

Reusable diagnostics live in `scripts/experiments/`. Throwaway repro code is inlined in the entry.

---

## Traps & settled questions

One line each; read this before starting on a scraper or pipeline problem.

- **Before re-drawing a labelled sample, check that its strata still exist in the DB.** SYS-36's `finance_reject` (finance + `rejected_filter` + no body) and `soft_save` populations are both 0 rows after SYS-35's re-queue and SYS-34's cleanup; a fresh `sample` silently drops them. Metadata drift (a category rewrite) is `label_filter_gold.py refresh` on the frozen file, never a re-draw. → [2026-09-21 Phase 0.5 entry](#2026-09-21--phase-05-sys-42-sys-43sys-44-dbt-32-worked-sys-45-proposed-on-sys-37)
- **A deny-list *substring* stem is a collision waiting for the next source's vocabulary -- word-bound it or don't add it.** `sport` matched `transportasi` (2026-09-19), and the `celeb` stem added by SYS-34 on 2026-09-20 matches INEWS's Sulawesi desk `celebes` (200 hard-news rows, `gate=deny`). Before adding a stem, run it against every live `(source, category)` (the gate-rule audit in the 2026-09-21 QA entry takes 5 s) and add the collision to the test. → SYS-42 (#137)
- **A committed fixture that carries DB metadata (`category`, `stratum`) goes stale the moment a repair script rewrites that metadata -- refresh it, don't trust the freeze date.** The SYS-36 gold sample was frozen 40 minutes before `fix_url_shaped_categories.py` rewrote 6,786 categories; 336 gold rows still carry URL categories and 178 hard-news rows sit in `mixed_bucket`. → SYS-44 (#139)
- **Stratifying by `(source, category, status)` cell on a corpus with syndicated mirror hosts draws the same article once per host.** INEWS's 126 regional "categories" put 319 rows / 164 distinct headlines into the gold set, 46 test rows leak from train and 8 headlines carry both labels. Dedup the population by normalised headline first; assert uniqueness on the output. → SYS-43 (#138)
- **"100% saved" after a re-queue is a recall number; read the precision side before calling the gate right.** The SYS-35 allow set is 85.7% news on the gold set (ANTARANEWS `Ekonomi` 33%, SINDONEWS `ekbis` 53%); the re-queued population was 20% soft and every row of it is now `saved`. → SYS-45 (#140)
- **Narrowing `_get_branches` to "the finance leaf" is not done until you have listed every finance-looking `<sitemap>` in the live index and decided about each one.** INEWS has three disjoint finance sub-sections (`finance/keuangan`, `finance/makro`, `finance/bisnis`; SRC-27 kept one on the false premise there was only one) and LIPUTAN6 has `saham` next to `bisnis` (SRC-05 kept one). Grep the live index for `ekonomi|bisnis|finan|keuangan|uang|saham|bursa|invest|makro|business|econ` and compare with `_get_branches(soup)` before closing any SRC branch ticket. → SRC-30 (#112) · SRC-31 (#113) · [2026-09-20 QA audit](#2026-09-20--qa-audit-of-the-src-27src-28dbt-29dbt-30-closures-with-a-fresh-eyes-pass-over-the-whole-2026-09-1920-batch-6-new-tickets)
- ~~SYS-31's category deny-list is allow-by-default and only knows the vocabulary that was in the DB on 2026-09-19.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-34, #115, code; cleanup pending owner):** the IDNTIMES vocabulary and every other soft name in the DB was sampled (3-4 random titles each) and added; `kriminal`/`edukasi` left (news); the service now WARNs the first time a source saves under a (source, category) pair it never saved under before, so the *next* vocabulary is seen on day one. **A category name is not evidence in either direction**: OKEZONE `muslim` (haji security, Wamenhaj) and `ototekno` (Meta subscriptions) look soft and are news; JPNN `donwori` looks like nothing and is a relationship-confession column with 24 saves. Sample before listing. → [2026-09-20 Phase 0 entry](#2026-09-20--phase-0-of-the-news-worthy-programme-sys-22-src-11-src-36src-41-sys-35sys-34-sys-33-sys-36)
- ~~SYS-32's URL-slug headline is a permanent placeholder, not a stopgap.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-33, #114, code; 486-row repair pending owner):** `Crawler._is_placeholder_headline` (headline == slug-derived text AND the slug ends in an id token) makes `_fetch_news_details` take og:title, and the service writes it back to `sitemaps.headline` for every fetched page, rejected ones included. **Equality with the slug alone is not the test**: 3,580 genuine title-cased, punctuation-free headlines on MEDIAINDONESIA/JPNN/INEWS/KOMPAS coincide with their own slug ("Keadilan Terkubur Longsor"); the id tail (`-hCa5`, `-2287141`) is what distinguishes the placeholder. → same entry
- **`backfill_sitemap_crawl_status.py` still applies only half of SYS-31** (category gate yes, margin check no -- DBT-29 ported one of the two); 35.9% of the rows it leaves `pending` are ones the live filter rejects. Its docstring says "Same decision as `is_important()`"; it is not, until DBT-31 lands. → DBT-31 (#116) · same entry
- **SRC-29's dead-backlog shape is INEWS-only, measured 2026-09-20:** 25 random eligible-backlog URLs per source on the 13 largest queues (VIVA, MEDIAINDONESIA, JPNN, ...) all answer `200` at 76-100%; the non-200s are timeouts/rate limits, not 404/410. Don't re-probe for this without a new reason. → same entry
- ~~The semantic filter's raw 0.35 threshold rejects 30-55% of every outlet's dedicated finance section, permanently~~ (world oil prices 0.254, Boeing shares 0.203, BI/QRIS 0.288). **Gate fixed 2026-09-20 (SYS-35, #118, code; 2,080-row re-queue pending owner):** `category_gate()` is symmetric -- a news desk (`NEWS_CATEGORIES`) is kept before the score, exactly as a soft vertical is rejected before it; only mixed buckets are scored. The score itself is untouched and still ~50% recall on finance text: the per-run `semantic-score recall on news categories` INFO line measures it until SYS-37 replaces it. **`news`/`berita`/`news-sitemap` are not on the allow-set (owner decision 2026-09-20, on SYS-35)** -- KUMPARAN `news` sampled 1-of-4 hard news (gallery, how-to, opinion beside it), VIVA `news-sitemap` 0-of-4; those are the buckets SYS-37's precision gate is measured on. → [2026-09-20 Phase 0 entry](#2026-09-20--phase-0-of-the-news-worthy-programme-sys-22-src-11-src-36src-41-sys-35sys-34-sys-33-sys-36) · [2026-09-20 VIVA audit](#2026-09-20--full-audit-of-viva-what-is-actually-in-the-39k-backlog-what-a-real-run-would-save-and-why-the-finance-section-itself-loses-42)
- **`re.sub` on a non-matching link returns its input: four crawlers have keyed branches by their full URL this way** (IDNTIMES SRC-14, VIVA SRC-33, CNN SRC-36, JPNN SRC-41 -- the last one was in the 09-20 fleet probe's `URL-KEYS` flag and nobody ticketed it). Use `re.match` + skip, expose one `branch_name_from_url(link)` per crawler, and repair stored rows through that same function (`scripts/fix_url_shaped_categories.py`), never a regex re-derived in the script. A regional-network key must carry the host (`bali/politik`): fifteen `politik` leaves collapse to one dict entry otherwise. → same entry
- **Do not run `task=sitemap`/`task=all` for VIVA until SRC-33 lands.** `_get_branches` returns 53 branches (24 `www` sections + 29 subdomains keyed by their full URL), the 39k backlog is 97.8% non-finance, and the newest-1,000 `full_text` window has zero `bisnis` rows. Before the first real run on any big untested backlog, do the dry run shape from the VIVA entry (real `batch_crawling_details` + real filter, nothing saved) -- it answers "what would this save" for ~200 fetches instead of ~130 junk production rows. → SRC-33 (#119) · same entry
- **`scripts/check_crawlers.py` never calls `save_sitemaps`/`save_newsdetails`/`save_chunk_data` — it is a read-only extraction sampler, not a save-path test.** "Verified with `check_crawlers.py`" only proves fetch+extraction works; a fix to sitemap parsing, dedup, or DTO construction (SYS-32's headline/timestamp fallback, SRC-16's two-level traversal) still needs one real `task=sitemap` run (cheap — no full-text fetch, no embedding) before it's actually proven end to end. → [2026-09-20 remediation-batch QA audit](#2026-09-20--qa-audit-of-the-2026-09-1920-remediation-batch-does-it-all-actually-hold)
- **A `Fixes #N` commit footer only auto-closes the issue if it lands on (or is merged to) `main` — `dev` never triggers it.** SYS-14 (#33) and DBT-07 (#34) were fixed and committed with proper footers on 2026-09-16 but stayed open on GitHub for two days since `dev` has never merged to `main`. Check `git log --oneline --all | grep <TICKET>` before assuming an open ticket needs new work. → [2026-09-18 P2-medium sweep](#2026-09-18--p2-medium-sweep-9-tickets-fixed-2-more-filed-and-fixed-along-the-way-ops-04-built-and-dbt-20-partially-resolved)
- **BISNIS's 403 is a genuine Cloudflare managed challenge (`Cf-Mitigated: challenge`, `<title>Just a moment...</title>`), confirmed 2026-09-19 across every path/subdomain tried** (`/sitemap-news.xml`, `robots.txt`'s `/sitemap.xml`, `/rss`, `finansial.bisnis.com`) -- not a header/UA gap. Modern headers (Chrome UA, `Accept-Language: id-ID`, `Referer`) made no difference, as expected for a JS challenge. Fixing this needs a real headless browser, and Cloudflare's modern challenges routinely fingerprint headless automation too -- success isn't guaranteed even with that investment. Don't re-try header tweaks; the next attempt should start from `core/page_loader/headless_page_loader.py` (currently unconfigured for Windows -- `EXECUTABLE_PATH` defaults to a macOS path) or accept BISNIS as unfixable without it. → SRC-03 (#14)
- **Changing the User-Agent does not fix any current sitemap failure.** All 32 sitemap URLs return the same status with the 2017 default UA and a modern Chrome UA. 403s (BISNIS, IDNTIMES) are server-side bot protection, 404s are dead URLs. → [2026-09-15 UA probe](#2026-09-15--user-agent-is-not-why-sitemaps-fail)
- **A "BROKEN" scraper is usually not a layout change.** Of 14 unhealthy outlets on 2026-09-15, only 2 were article-layout changes; 5 were dead URLs, 2 were 403s, 1 TLS, 3 sitemap-format changes, 1 crawler crash. Check the sitemap URL and index format first. → [2026-09-15 health check](#2026-09-15--full-health-check-of-all-32-crawlers)
- ~~LIPUTAN6's sitemap index format changed — `_get_branches` finds nothing.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-05, #16):** the live index moved from `<section>/sitemap_news.xml` to `<section>/sitemap.xml`; `_get_branches` now matches the new pattern and keeps `bisnis`. Also found while verifying: liputan6.com's article pages 404 with `?page=all` appended (the base `Crawler._get_link` appends it to every URL except JPNN) -- overrode `_get_link` for this outlet too, or the branch fix alone would have looked like "listing OK, extraction 0/2" (`PARTIAL`) instead of `OK`. And `_get_reporter_from_text` was a bare `pass` (implicit `None`) -- fixed as SRC-25 (#105), same shape as SRC-21. **A branch-parsing fix is not "done" until `check_crawlers.py` shows OK, not just non-zero links -- the link-fetch step can still be broken separately.**
- ~~"Dead sitemap URL" is not the diagnosis until you have read `_get_branches`.~~ ANTARANEWS and SUARA never parsed the index — their branch lists were hardcoded in `antaranews_branch.py` / `suara_branch.py` and the enum URL was only a liveness probe. **Both fixed 2026-09-19: SRC-13 (#91)** -- `URL.ANTARANEWS` now points at a live host (`rss/ekonomi.xml`), `_get_branches` restricted to the 4 `ekonomi*` feeds (55 of the network's 60 feeds are off-topic per SYS-31), and the category label fixed (was the constant string `"ANTARA News"` for every branch, regardless of which of the 4 feeds it came from). **SRC-12 (#90)** -- see the SUARA entry above. Check the newest listed date, not the count, remains true generally. → [2026-09-19 SRC-13 fix](#2026-09-19--src-13-fix-do-the-4-ekonomi-feeds-and-the-category-fix-actually-work) · OPS-17 (#89)
- ~~TIRTO, TEMPO and BATAMPOS (Yoast) serve `lastmod`-only sitemaps — no `news:title`, no `news:publication_date`.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SYS-32, #94):** `Crawler._get_timestamp` falls back to `<lastmod>`; `_get_sitemap` derives a placeholder headline from the URL slug when there's no `news:title` (SitemapDTO.headline is pydantic-validated `str` — a bare `None` was never going to reach `save_sitemaps` as a `NotNullViolation`, it fails DTO construction inside `_get_sitemap`'s own try/except first, so the real prior behavior was "silently dropped at INFO", not the DB exception the ticket described); an entry with neither date source is dropped up front with one `WARNING` per branch (`SitemapScrapeBatch.skipped_no_date`, logged by `crawl_sitemaps` next to `new_count`) instead of N per-row log lines. `_get_news_details` prefers a real `og:title`/`<title>` over an empty sitemap headline. Live-verified against TIRTO's real `sitemap/ekonomi/sitemap-ekonomi-post-september-2026.xml`: 275/275 entries now build a valid `SitemapDTO` with a WIB timestamp and a readable (if slug-derived) headline, 0 skipped. → [2026-09-19 SYS-32 fix](#2026-09-19--sys-32-fix-does-a-lastmodplaceholder-headline-fallback-actually-save-tirtos-rows) · SRC-16 (#95) still needs its own `_get_branches` rewrite before TIRTO's sitemap crawl reaches this leaf at all
- ~~INEWS's TLS failure is the venv's `certifi==2022.12.7` (no Sectigo R46 root), not the site.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (DBT-28, #98; closed SRC-04, #15):** `pip install -U certifi` (2022.12.7 -> 2026.7.22), pin updated in `requirements.txt`. No code change, no `verify=False`. `check_crawlers.py --website INEWS` now fetches the sitemap (200) but reports `FALLBACK` (site extractor dead, rescued by trafilatura) -- a separate pre-existing issue, filed as SRC-19 (#99). Never reach for `verify=False`; check the bundle first. → same entry
- ~~KAPANLAGI has no economy section and BATAMPOS no usable sitemap — retire them rather than fix them.~~ **Retired 2026-09-20 (SRC-17/SRC-18, #96/#97; user decision):** removed from `WebsiteName`, `URL`, `CRAWLER_DICT`, `WEB_URL_DICT`; `newscrawler/infrastructure/datasource/scrapers/{kapanlagi,batampos}/` deleted; `__main__.py`'s `HEALTHY` list updated to include every outlet fixed 2026-09-19/20 (ANTARANEWS, TIRTO, SUARA, LIPUTAN6, MERDEKA, TEMPO, IDNTIMES, INEWS). Pipeline is now ~30 outlets, not 32. ~~IDNTIMES is no longer 403 at the root (`/sitemap.xml` is 200, only the old path is blocked).~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-14, #92):** `URL.IDNTIMES` -> `/sitemap.xml`; `_get_branches`' `re.sub` used to return a non-matching link unchanged, so every `sitemap-web.xml`/`sitemap-image.xml` entry became a branch keyed by its own full URL -- now `re.match` skips anything that isn't `<section>/sitemap-news.xml`. 16 clean branches incl. `business`, 0 full-URL keys. Removed from SRC-03 (#14)'s outlet list. Site extractor dead on the current layout -- filed SRC-22 (#102). → same entry
- ~~SUARA's 20 hardcoded regional sitemaps are frozen at 2022-10-23.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-12, #90):** `URL.SUARA` now points at the real index (`https://www.suara.com/sitemap.xml`), `_get_branches` parses it and keeps `bisnis`'s `sitemap-news.xml` leaf, `SuaraNetwork`/`suara_branch.py` deleted, the `category=None` special-case in `Crawler._get_sitemap` removed (category is now a real section name). **`SuaraCrawler.__init__` had never set `self.website_url` at all** -- `_get_branches` used to ignore the fetched index completely, so nothing ever needed it; `check_crawlers.py` caught this immediately as `get_soup(None)` before the fix was even complete. Live-verified: `check_crawlers.py --website SUARA` lists 200 links dated today back to 2026-09-15 (not 2022). Site extractor is dead on the current layout (`article.detail-content` no longer matches) -- filed as SRC-20 (#100), same class as SRC-08/SRC-19. → SRC-12 (#90)
- ~~"OK" in `check_crawlers.py` can hide a dead selector.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (OPS-02, #10):** `crawler.py` now records which extractor won in `meta_data["extractor"]` ("site"/"trafilatura"/"newspaper"/`None`), persisted on `articles.meta_data`; `check_crawlers.py` reports `FALLBACK` (needs attention, non-fatal -- excluded from `UNHEALTHY`/the exit code, included in `NEEDS_ATTENTION`) when every successful sample came from a fallback. Live-confirmed: CNBC, CNN, KONTAN, PIKIRANRAKYAT all now show `FALLBACK` instead of `OK`. → [2026-09-16 OPS-02 fix](#2026-09-16--ops-02-fix-does-tracking-which-extractor-won-catch-the-four-known-fallback-sources)
- **KUMPARAN and OKEZONE (SRC-07, #18) work again once SYS-01/SYS-02 landed, exactly as predicted -- both FALLBACK, not BROKEN.** By 2026-09-19 this makes 8 outlets (SRC-08's 4 + SRC-19/20/22/23/24/26) whose site-specific selector is dead and trafilatura is doing all the work. Don't file another per-outlet ticket for this pattern -- it's DBT-05 (#24)'s call now (extraction strategy generally), not a per-selector fix.
- ~~A site extractor returning `[]` is NOT rescued by the fallback chain; only `None`/`""` is.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (#2):** any result that is empty or under `MIN_ARTICLE_CHARS` (default 200) now falls through site → trafilatura → newspaper, and a failed fallback never replaces a shorter real result. → [2026-09-16 verification](#2026-09-16--does-the-2-fallback-fix-rescue-kumparan-live)
- ~~`get_session()` swallows exceptions — a DB write that "succeeded" may not have.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-03):** it now rolls back and re-raises. Call sites that must tolerate a DB error (`crawl_log` writes, web UI, `check_crawlers --log-db`) have their own try/except. ~~But a DB outage still exits 0~~ — the constructor query that made it exit non-zero was removed by SYS-07. **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-06, #7):** `process_event` now catches `Exception` (not `BaseException`, so `KeyboardInterrupt`/`SystemExit` propagate untouched), logs with `logger.exception` at ERROR, and re-raises — an uncaught exception in `__main__.py` exits non-zero. ~~And `save_sitemaps` still swallows a failed `COMMIT` and returns phantom IDs.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-13, #32):** the try/except around `session.commit()` is gone; a failed commit now propagates through `get_session()`. → [2026-09-16 verification](#2026-09-16--does-re-raising-in-get_session-make-a-db-outage-visible-sys-03) · [2026-09-16 re-audit](#2026-09-16--qa-re-audit-is-a-db-outage-still-loud-after-sys-07-and-what-about-the-commit-path-sys-03) · [2026-09-16 SYS-13/14/DBT-07 fix verification](#2026-09-16--sys-13-sys-14-dbt-07-fix-verification)
- ~~`task=all` re-fetches every URL the sitemap lists, every run, and duplicates their `articles` rows~~ (since SYS-07 removed the date gate — `crawl_website("all")` passes the full `save_sitemaps` result into `crawl_newsdetails(specific_sitemaps=…)`, bypassing the no-article/cooldown filter). **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-07 reopened, #8):** `crawl_website("all")` now calls `crawl_newsdetails(website_name)` with no `specific_sitemaps`, so it loads the filtered (no-article-row AND cooldown-elapsed) set from the DB instead — same path `task=full_text` already used. `crawl_log.article_count` for `task='sitemap'` now reports `new_entries_count` (via `SitemapSaveResult`, a `list` subclass that carries the count without changing what any existing caller sees), not "entries listed". ~~`articles.sitemap_id` has no unique index.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (DBT-07, #34)** — code landed in 5b26ca8 and the migration was applied to `newsaggregator` later the same day (0 duplicates, `articles_sitemap_id_uq` present). ~~Don't trust "Saved batch N" log lines from `save_newsdetails`: any per-row DB error silently discards the whole batch.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-15, #36):** each row's insert now runs in its own `session.begin_nested()` savepoint, matching `save_sitemaps`; a bad row rolls back only itself instead of poisoning the batch. → [2026-09-16 SYS-15 fix verification](#2026-09-16--sys-15-fix-does-a-per-row-savepoint-stop-a-bad-row-from-dropping-the-whole-batch) → [2026-09-16 re-audit](#2026-09-16--qa-re-audit-does-taskall-re-crawl-already-articled-sitemaps-now-that-the-date-gate-is-gone-sys-07) · [2026-09-16 SYS-13/14/DBT-07 fix verification](#2026-09-16--sys-13-sys-14-dbt-07-fix-verification) · [2026-09-16 QA re-audit of SYS-13/14/DBT-07 fixes](#2026-09-16--qa-re-audit-of-sys-1314dbt-07-fixes)
- **A 200 status is not proof the page is the article.** emitennews.com 307-redirects missing/unpublished articles to `/404`, which returns HTTP 200 with the home page; trafilatura "extracts" the ticker tape (1.4 k chars, passes the semantic filter). **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-14, #33):** `get_soup` now rejects a 200 whose final `response.url`/`history` or `<link rel=canonical>` matches an error-page pattern (`/404`, `/not-found`, `/error`). Still run `scripts/experiments/probe_soft404.py --website X` before trusting a health-check `ok`, since the pattern-match is necessarily a heuristic. → SYS-14 (#33) · [2026-09-16 re-audit](#2026-09-16--qa-re-audit-emitennews-serves-its-home-page-with-http-200-for-missing-articles-src-01) · [2026-09-16 SYS-13/14/DBT-07 fix verification](#2026-09-16--sys-13-sys-14-dbt-07-fix-verification)
- **EMITENNEWS article pages carry no machine-readable date** (no `datePublished`, `<time>`, or date meta), so `<lastmod>` cannot be cross-checked against publication date from the page. Don't try; watch `sitemaps` for duplicate links per source instead. → same entry
- **The integration suite needs a clean `newsaggregator_test`.** Tests commit fixture rows they never delete; after a few months DS01/DS11 fail on leftovers and the backfill cleanup hits an FK violation. Truncate the test DB (guarded on `current_database()`) before trusting a red run. `test_backfill_pipeline.py` is additionally blocked by SYS-10 (#28) — `scripts/backfill_chunks.py`'s own `EMBEDDING_MODEL=nomic-embed-text` 401s against HF instead of using the pipeline's `EMBEDDING_MODEL_HF`. → same entry · DBT-06 (#29) · SYS-10 (#28)
- ~~A missed crawl run cannot be backfilled from the sitemap because of the pre-dedup date gate.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-07), then reopened the same day:** the gate is gone; `save_sitemaps`' `(link, posted_at)` dedup is the only filter, so re-listed older entries are inserted — but the gate was also what kept `task=all` from re-crawling everything (see the `task=all` trap above). `backfill_missing_articles.py` still only re-crawls sitemaps already in the DB — to recover a missed *sitemap* window, just run the sitemap task while the site still lists it. → [2026-09-16 verification](#2026-09-16--does-removing-the-date-gate-unblock-emitennews-and-backfills-sys-07)

Its only unique file was `scripts/migrate_posted_at.sql`, already carried over. → [2026-09-16 worktree check](#2026-09-16--is-the-orphaned-worktree-branch-still-needed)
- **Broken crawlers on 2026-06-02 were verified non-transient** (fail single-threaded with a 90 s timeout), so re-running "just in case" is wasted time — fix the root cause. → [2026-06-02 entry](#2026-06-02--are-the-17-broken-crawlers-transient)
- ~~`articles.extracted_text` is a Postgres array literal, not prose.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-18, #39):** the write path now joins paragraphs with `\n\n` before the value reaches the driver; migrated all 11,889 existing rows to prose on `newsaggregator` and reset the 6 already-extracted rows' `knowledge_extracted` flag so they re-extract from clean text. The stray `\"`/`","` artifacts in 25/32 `entity_mentions.context_snippet` rows are untouched by this migration (they were captured before the fix) — a pre-existing snippet, not a live bug. → [2026-09-16 deep audit](#2026-09-16--qa-deep-audit-of-the-whole-pipeline-19-new-tickets) · [2026-09-16 SYS-18 fix](#2026-09-16--sys-18-fix-does-joining-paragraphs-at-the-write-boundary-stop-the-array-literal-corruption)
- ~~FAISS membership is not proof the `chunks` rows exist.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-17, #38):** `save_chunk_data` now dedups on `SQLAlchemyChunkDataSource.get_existing_sitemap_ids` (SQL), not FAISS; `ChunkFAISSDatasource._embed_batch` recovers the *real* embedding_id for anything already in the index (keyed on sitemap_id+level+index) instead of returning `embedding_id=None`. This makes the whole thing self-healing on retry. Repaired the 152 WARTAEKONOMI orphans via `scripts/backfill_orphan_chunks.py` (re-chunks from `articles.extracted_text`, no re-crawl needed since SYS-18). When checking "is X embedded", check `chunks`, not the docstore — still true; the mechanism just changed to actually enforce it. → [2026-09-16 SYS-17 fix](#2026-09-16--sys-17-fix-does-sql-based-dedup-repair-the-faiss-orphan-chunks) · OPS-04 (#52)
- ~~`newsletter_digests` does not exist on either DB.~~ **Fully fixed 2026-09-16 (OPS-05, #53):** `migrate_phase3.sql` was applied (step 1, same day), but `task=newsletter` *still* couldn't complete -- a second, independent bug (SYS-23, #59: `:categories::jsonb` never recognized as a bind param by SQLAlchemy's `text()`) meant `save_digest` had never once executed successfully, migration or no migration. Both are fixed now: `scripts/migrate.py` tracks every migration in a `schema_migrations` table (idempotent, run it any time), and a real `task=newsletter` run against production data (2026-06-02, 589 articles) completed end-to-end and wrote a real `newsletter_digests` row. **Lesson: "the table now exists" is not the same claim as "the feature that writes to it works" — verify the actual write path, not just its prerequisite.** → OPS-05 (#53) fixed · SYS-23 (#59) fixed · OPS-06 (#54) still open
- **73 % of `sitemaps` (73,364) are "attempted, no article" and will be re-fetched every 7 days forever** — filter rejections have no terminal state. Any per-source "N sitemaps, few articles" number must be read against this before concluding a scraper is broken. → SYS-16 (#37)
- ~~A typo in `CRAWL_TASK` is a green run that does nothing.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (SYS-21, #42):** `crawl_websites_in_batch` validates the task and every website name before any work starts, and `crawl_website` has an `else: raise`. ~~The third silent input — an empty website list for `sitemap`/`full_text`/`all` (what `process_event({"task": "all"})` produces when the `"website"` key is absent) — was still `ok=0 failed=0`, zero service calls, exit 0.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (SYS-30, #83):** it now raises `ValueError("task 'all' needs at least one website ...")`, which SYS-06 turns into a non-zero exit. → SYS-21 (#42) · SYS-30 (#83)
- ~~`check_crawlers.py` is not scheduled.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (OPS-01, #9)**, missed-window gap **fixed 2026-09-17 (OPS-01 reopened, #9):** a Windows Task Scheduler job **"NewsAggregator Crawler Health Check"** runs `scripts/run_scheduled_health_check.py` daily at 05:30 WIB (`schtasks /Query /TN "NewsAggregator Crawler Health Check" /FO LIST /V`), which wraps `check_crawlers.py --quiet --log-db` and sends a Telegram alert via `scripts/notify_telegram.py` on any unhealthy source; Telegram credentials landed in `.env` 2026-09-17. The task now also has `-StartWhenAvailable`/`-WakeToRun` set (were `False`/`False`), so a missed 05:30 window runs late on next boot/wake instead of being skipped outright, and `run_scheduled_health_check.py` checks the gap since the last `task='health_check'` `crawl_log` row itself on every run, alerting if it exceeds 36h (`OPERATIONS.md` → "Detect a missed/broken schedule" has the same query, checkable without opening Task Scheduler). `LogonType=Interactive` was left as-is (re-registering with stored credentials is a separate, bigger change). Never read "no alert today" as "all sources healthy" without also checking that gap query — a scheduler that stops firing entirely (deleted task, expired creds) is still silent otherwise. **Reopened again 2026-09-17 (second QA pass):** the machine is a laptop and the task still has the defaults `DisallowStartIfOnBatteries=true` / `StopIfGoingOnBatteries=true` — a 05:30 on battery is a *condition not met*, which `StartWhenAvailable` does not queue, so it is a third silent-skip mode after off/asleep; and no unattended run had ever happened when the ticket was closed (task registered 05:47, after the first 05:30). Fix is `-AllowStartIfOnBatteries -DontStopIfGoingOnBatteries`; the Done-when is an *observed* unattended run. **Observed and closed 2026-09-18:** first unattended run fired at 05:30:02 WIB, 32 `crawl_log` rows at 22:32 UTC, Telegram received. Still unexercised: catch-up after a genuinely missed window (a miss surfaces via the 36 h gap check at the *next* run, ~48 h, not silently) and `LogonType=Interactive`. The `Microsoft-Windows-TaskScheduler/Operational` log is disabled on this machine, so `LastRunTime` + a single `crawl_log` batch is the only scheduled-vs-manual evidence. ~~And note the alert is not yet *actionable* daily — it names the same 17 known-broken outlets every morning~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (OPS-13, #76):** the alert now leads with NEW / CHANGED / RECOVERED against each outlet's previous `crawl_log` row and summarises the unchanged known-broken set as a count; a no-change day says so in its first line, and `HEALTH_ALERT_ON_NO_CHANGE=false` can mute those. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--dbt-06-sys-04-reopened-ops-01-reopened-making-the-integration-gate-trustworthy-again-and-closing-the-two-done-when-gaps-from-the-qa-pass) · [2026-09-17 second QA pass](#2026-09-17--qa-re-audit-second-pass-of-the-7-ticket-fix-batch-dbt-06-sys-04r-ops-01r-sys-25-ops-09-src-10-src-09) · [2026-09-18 observation](#2026-09-18--ops-01-first-unattended-scheduled-health-check-run)
- ~~Knowledge extraction can be starved by 50 unparseable articles~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-19, #40):** `articles.knowledge_extract_attempts`/`last_extract_error` track failures; `get_articles_pending_extraction` excludes `attempts >= KNOWLEDGE_EXTRACT_MAX_ATTEMPTS` (default 3) and orders `attempts ASC, posted_at DESC` so a failing row sinks behind fresh ones. Per-article entity writes + `mark_article_extracted` are now one transaction (`atomic_extraction_session`) — a mid-article crash rolls back everything for that article, not just some of the mentions. → [2026-09-16 SYS-19 fix](#2026-09-16--sys-19-fix-does-attempt-tracking-stop-a-poison-article-from-burning-a-gemini-call-forever)
- ~~VIVA: 37,381 sitemaps attempted, 0 articles saved, healthy extractor~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-08, #26):** `Crawler` is a plain class, not an ABC, so `@abstractmethod` on `_get_reporter_from_text` is decorative -- `VivaCrawler` never overrode it and silently inherited the base no-op (`None`). Combined with `NewsDetailsModel.reporter: List[str] = None` (a Python default, not `Optional[List[str]]`), every single VIVA article crashed `NewsDetailsModel` construction inside `save_newsdetails`'s per-row `try/except`, forever. **`tribun_crawler.py` has the identical gap (SRC-09, #57, not yet fixed) — TRIBUN has 0 sitemaps in the DB so far, but will hit the exact same 0-articles-forever bug the moment it's actually crawled.** When a "healthy per `check_crawlers.py`, 0 articles saved" source shows up, check whether its crawler overrides every abstract method before assuming the filter or extractor is at fault — `grep -L _get_reporter_from_text` across every `scrapers/<site>/<site>_crawler.py` finds every other silently-broken source in one shot. → [2026-09-16 SYS-08 fix](#2026-09-16--sys-08-fix-does-fixing-reporternone-let-viva-save-articles)
- ~~73% of `sitemaps` (73,364) are "attempted, no article" and will be re-fetched every 7 days forever~~ **Fixed 2026-09-16 (SYS-16, #37):** `sitemaps.crawl_status` (`pending`/`rejected_filter`/`empty_extraction`/`saved`) now distinguishes a permanent semantic-filter rejection from a transient empty-extraction failure; `load_all_sitemaps` excludes `rejected_filter` permanently. Backfilled the existing rows via `scripts/backfill_sitemap_crawl_status.py` (headline-only re-classification, since the body was never saved) -- **except VIVA**, whose 21,788 initial classifications were reverted to `pending` because SYS-08 (VIVA: healthy extractor, 0 articles) is still open and this heuristic can't tell "off-topic" apart from "a systemic bug made every extraction look empty" — don't re-run the backfill for VIVA until SYS-08 resolves. Real queue: 73,364 → 48,401 (24,963 non-VIVA rows now correctly excluded). Any per-source "N sitemaps, few articles" number must still be read against the *pending* count, not raw attempted-no-article. → [2026-09-16 SYS-16 fix](#2026-09-16--sys-16-fix-does-a-crawl_status-terminal-marker-stop-the-73-re-fetch-forever-loop)
- ~~`crawl_log.article_count` for `task='full_text'` over-reports~~ **Fixed 2026-09-17 (SYS-04 reopened, #5):** `save_newsdetails` now `RETURNING`s the sitemap_ids the upsert actually committed (a skip via `on_conflict_do_nothing` returns no row, same as a row that raised inside its savepoint), and that list -- not `len(to_save)` -- drives both `saved_total` and the `'saved'` status stamp. Live-verified: an all-FK-violation batch now returns `[]` and stamps nothing; a half-good batch returns only the committed id. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--dbt-06-sys-04-reopened-ops-01-reopened-making-the-integration-gate-trustworthy-again-and-closing-the-two-done-when-gaps-from-the-qa-pass)
- ~~`tests/integration` is red by default~~ **Fixed 2026-09-17 (DBT-06, #29):** a session-scoped autouse `TRUNCATE ... RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE` fixture (guarded on `current_database()='newsaggregator_test'`) now wipes the test DB before every `pytest tests/integration` run, `test_backfill_pipeline.py`'s cleanup deletes by resolved `sitemap_id` instead of a content pattern that missed empty-text rows, and DS01 asserts by unique link instead of "last 3 rows by id". `test_backfill_pipeline.py` itself is `xfail(strict=False)` module-wide pending SYS-10 (`nomic-embed-text` 401) — that one still needs a real fix, not just a green suite. Verified green twice in a row from a DB that started with 400+ leftover fixture rows. **SYS-16's and OPS-05's regression guards are readable again** — don't let this drift back; if `tests/integration` goes red, treat it as a real regression, not baseline noise. → [2026-09-17 DBT-06/SYS-04/OPS-01 fix](#2026-09-17--dbt-06-sys-04-reopened-ops-01-reopened-making-the-integration-gate-trustworthy-again-and-closing-the-two-done-when-gaps-from-the-qa-pass)
- **A passing test is not a regression guard — mutate the fix and re-run.** The 2026-09-17 QA pass caught 15 of 16 re-introduced P1 bugs from `tests/unit` alone, but SYS-16's central `crawl_status != 'rejected_filter'` clause could be deleted with all 296 unit tests still green. The second pass the same day: 17 of 20, and the three survivors (SYS-04's `RETURNING` line in `save_newsdetails`, twice; `backfill_orphan_chunks.py`'s level-aware WHERE) also survive `tests/integration` — a "live-verified in a REPL" claim in this log is not a guard until it is a test file (DBT-19, #69). → [2026-09-17 QA sign-off](#2026-09-17--qa-sign-off-pass-over-the-13-closed-p1-tickets) · [2026-09-17 second QA pass](#2026-09-17--qa-re-audit-second-pass-of-the-7-ticket-fix-batch-dbt-06-sys-04r-ops-01r-sys-25-ops-09-src-10-src-09)
- **A manual `check_crawlers.py --website X` run overwrites that day's full JSON report.** `crawler_health/<date>.json` is date-keyed and opened with `"w"`; `crawl_log` still has the full run, but the per-outlet `avg_chars`/`extractors`/`note` fields only live in the JSON. Verify SRC fixes with `--report-dir` pointed somewhere else until OPS-10 (#66) lands.
- **`task=extract_knowledge` and `task=newsletter` write no `crawl_log` row**, and since OPS-09 an API-level Gemini failure is a per-article WARNING with nothing persisted — a dead `GEMINI_API_KEY` is a run that "completes" with zero progress and zero signal in any table. Check `pending_retryable` is actually *moving* between runs, not just that it is non-zero. → OPS-11 (#67)
- **The 187 duplicate FAISS vectors and the 9,447 VIVA sitemaps with chunks but no `articles` row are June-2026 artifacts**, confirmed by `chunks.created_at`, not caused by SYS-17 or the orphan repair. Already tracked (DBT-09, SYS-08). Don't re-file them.
- ~~FAISS `save_local` rewrites the full index non-atomically after every batch.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (DBT-10, #45):** `ChunkFAISSDatasource._atomic_save_local` writes into a sibling temp dir, then per file copies the live one to `*.bak` and swaps the new one in with `os.replace` -- pkl before faiss, so a crash between the two swaps leaves (old faiss, new pkl), which is still loadable (add_documents only appends, so the old index's internal ids all still resolve in the new mapping) instead of a mismatched pair. The backup step had to be a *copy*, not a move -- my first version moved the old file to `.bak` then replaced it, and a simulated crash on the replace step left the live file **missing entirely** (caught by my own test, not by inspection). **"Reduce write frequency" from the same ticket was deliberately not done** -- that needs touching the delicate `_get_existing_embedding_ids`/dedup logic SYS-17/DBT-09/SYS-26 already hardened across several P1s; not risking it for a performance-only change. **When a two-step swap can fail between steps, the failure-injection test that actually forces the crash window is the one that finds the off-by-one in your own reasoning** -- reasoning about safety on paper missed the copy-vs-move distinction; the test with a patched `os.replace` didn't. → [2026-09-19 DBT-10 fix](#2026-09-19--dbt-10-fix-does-the-atomic-swap-survive-a-crash-mid-swap-and-does-the-safe-order-actually-matter)
- **OPS-04 (#52)'s `scripts/check_store_consistency.py` already exists and was never closed.** 2026-09-19 re-check: 2/9 checks nonzero, both `KNOWN` (the VIVA debris above, at/below baseline), 0 `GROWING` -- this ticket's literal done-when ("runs clean") is really "no new divergence", per the script's own OK/KNOWN/GROWING design. The `--repair` mode from the ticket's fix list is still unbuilt -- deliberately not building a data-mutating tool for the VIVA debris before deciding what to actually do with it (delete? re-crawl? leave+flag?), same shape as SYS-31's pending decision. Check for "already built, ticket never closed" before assuming a fix list item needs fresh work.
- **Ollama per-article embedding was replaced on purpose** (sequential HTTP round-trips, poor vectors for long articles). Don't reintroduce it; in-process `sentence-transformers` batching is the design. → `PRD.md` §6.5
- ~~A sitemap with any `chunks` row is treated as fully saved~~ **Fixed 2026-09-17 (SYS-25, #62):** `save_chunk_data` dedups on `(sitemap_id, chunk_level)`, not `sitemap_id` alone -- a sitemap missing just its L1 or L2 chunks used to be unrepairable (both the dedup and `backfill_orphan_chunks.py` treated any existing row as "done"). 29 production MEDIAINDONESIA rows repaired; `no_l1`/`no_l2` query in OPERATIONS.md now reads 0. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--sys-25-ops-09-src-10-the-three-new-tickets-from-the-qa-pass)
- **A Gemini API error (network/timeout/5xx/429/auth) is not the same failure class as an unusable response (safety block/bad JSON) -- don't count both the same way against a per-article retry cap.** Fixed 2026-09-17 (OPS-09, #63): `GeminiTransientError` is raised only when the API call itself never returned a response; `knowledge_extract_attempts` and the daily call count are untouched for it, so an outage can't retire the queue. `scripts/reset_extraction_attempts.py` brings back anything retired before this fix. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--sys-25-ops-09-src-10-the-three-new-tickets-from-the-qa-pass)
- **Two elements sharing one CSS class is a live trap for `find("tag", {"class": ...})`.** IDXCHANNEL's byline `<a class="text-body--2 creator">` and its timestamp `<div class="text-body--2">` share a class; searching for the class without the more specific one (`creator`) silently grabs whichever tag the search happens to match. Fixed 2026-09-17 (SRC-10, #65); `Crawler._looks_like_a_name` (base class) now also rejects any reporter candidate that's purely digits or date-shaped, across every source, not just this one. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--sys-25-ops-09-src-10-the-three-new-tickets-from-the-qa-pass)
- **`chunks` now has a unique index, so a duplicate key in one `save_chunk_data` call is a lost batch, not a silent extra row.** DBT-09's `_embed_batch` guard only covers the new-chunk branch; the already-in-FAISS branch still returns both copies → `UniqueViolation` → the whole batch (up to 100 sitemaps) gets no `chunks`/`articles` rows for the cooldown window and `crawl_log` says `completed`. Live `task=all`/`full_text` batches are PK-distinct, so today only `specific_sitemaps` callers and repair scripts can trigger it — but that includes DBT-20's future repair. Fix SYS-26 (#71) before any repair that pushes already-embedded sitemaps through `save_chunk_data`. → [2026-09-17 third QA pass](#2026-09-17--qa-audit-third-pass-of-the-post-batch-work-ops-01-dbt-17-ops-04-dbt-20-dbt-09)
- **"Full suite: N passed" means `tests/unit` unless it says otherwise — run `tests/integration` too, every time, and doubly so for a ticket that adds a `scripts/migrate_*.sql` file.** DBT-09 closed with the integration gate red (migration applied to `newsaggregator`, never to `newsaggregator_test`); the guard test named the exact fix and nobody saw it. → DBT-22 (#73) · same entry
- **To prove a `FAISS.delete()` didn't misalign vectors from text, compare `index.reconstruct(i)` to a fresh embedding of `docstore[index_to_docstore_id[i]].page_content`.** Checking `page_content` alone proves nothing — text and vectors are separate structures. (Post-DBT-09: cosine 1.0000 on 600 samples; the store is fine.) → same entry
- ~~TEMPO's `ERROR: timeout after 60s` in the health check is SRC-06, not a slow site.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-06, #17):** `_get_branches` now targets `ekonomi-sitemap.xml` directly from the fetched index instead of fetching all 28 `-sitemap.xml` leaves up front (155 s) and mis-reading each one's first `<loc>` (an article) as the branch link. `get_news_in_bulk` -> `batch_crawling_sitemap` now runs in **0.9 s**, 200/200 valid rows (needed SYS-32's lastmod fallback, also closed by this verification -- TEMPO's leaf is lastmod-only, same as TIRTO's). → [2026-09-18 TEMPO probe](#2026-09-18--tempo-where-do-the-60-s-go) · SRC-06 (#17)
- ~~MERDEKA's branch sitemap format changed.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-15, #93):** it never had -- `_get_branches` ignored the fetched `soup` entirely and handed `{"news": URL.MERDEKA.value}` (the index URL) to `_scrape`, which looks for `<url>` and an index has `<sitemap>`, so it was always 0 links regardless of what the site served. Now parses `soup.find_all("sitemap")` and keeps `uang`'s leaf -- same shape as SRC-12's SUARA fix. 100 fresh rows live-verified. MERDEKA's half of SRC-06 (#17) closed by reference; TEMPO stays open there (its `_get_branches` has a different bug -- see the TEMPO entry above -- plus needs SYS-32's lastmod fallback). **When a branch parses to 0 `<url>`, check whether `_get_branches` even looked at `soup` before assuming the site's format changed.**
- ~~TIRTO's sitemap is a dead URL.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SRC-16, #95):** the live sitemap is a genuinely *two-level*, `lastmod`-only structure -- `URL.TIRTO` (`sitemap/news.xml`) lists per-section monthly sub-indexes (`insider/ekonomi.xml`, `insider/bisnis.xml`, 40 others off-topic), each of which lists one `<sitemap>` per month. `_get_branches` now fetches `ekonomi`'s and `bisnis`'s sub-index and picks each one's newest monthly leaf by comparing `<lastmod>` (not by assuming list order, though the live site does return newest-first). Required SYS-32's lastmod fallback (already landed) for the leaf sitemaps themselves, which have no `news:title`/`news:publication_date` at all. 286 live rows across both sections, 0 skipped. → [2026-09-19 SRC-16 fix](#2026-09-19--src-16-fix-does-the-two-level-lastmod-only-traversal-actually-work-end-to-end)
- ~~`crawl_log` `health_check` rows have `started_at = finished_at`~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (OPS-12, #75)** for rows with `log_id > 308` (written from 06:14 WIB on): `check_one` stamps its own aware-UTC window and `log_to_db` binds both columns explicitly. The 128 older rows keep their zero durations — read `finished_at` on those. **Bind timestamps to `crawl_log` as timezone-aware datetimes, never `datetime.now()`:** the columns are `timestamp without time zone` filled by `NOW()` on a UTC server, and an aware value gets the same session-TZ conversion, while a naive local `now()` would land 7 h off and break the 36 h gap check. → [2026-09-18 OPS-12/OPS-13 fix](#2026-09-18--ops-12--ops-13-per-outlet-timestamps-and-a-delta-first-health-alert)
- **The health alert is a per-run sample, so a one-off flap is reported as `NEW` on the day and `RECOVERED` the next.** Observed 2026-09-18 within one hour: IDXCHANNEL `BROKEN: first branch scraped 0 article links` after 21 s under 8-way concurrency, `OK` in 1.6 s on a solo re-check. Settle a surprising `NEW` with `check_crawlers.py --website X --diff-db --report-dir <scratch>` (no `--log-db`) before filing an SRC ticket; multi-run suppression was deliberately not built. → same entry
- ~~A fix that changes how `posted_at` is derived is a dedup-key change.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (SYS-29, #84):** `save_sitemaps` now dedups on `link` alone (a URL is the article's identity; the date never was), looks stored rows up by `link IN (...)` instead of a `posted_at` window, and `sitemaps_link_uq` enforces the same key in both DBs. The old `(link, int(posted_at))` key would have inserted 4,024 duplicate rows on the next crawl (3,817 from SYS-11's +1-day WIB shift, 207 from OKEZONE/GRIDID re-stamping `news:publication_date`). The 2 pre-existing duplicate links were collapsed with `scripts/dedupe_sitemap_links.py`. Still true in general: **grep for every lookup key containing a value before changing its derivation.** `scripts/experiments/probe_sitemap_dedup_drift.py` now calls the real `find_existing_by_link` lookup instead of re-deriving the key by hand, so it cannot drift from the code again. → [2026-09-18 QA audit](#2026-09-18--qa-audit-of-the-p2-medium-sweep-sys-26-sys-102728-sys-11-sys-21-sys-09-ops-04-dbt-20-ops-03-ops-1213-1-p1-filed-1-reopened-5-more-tickets) · [2026-09-18 SYS-29 fix](#2026-09-18--sys-29-make-the-sitemap-dedup-key-the-link-alone-and-enforce-it)
- ~~`scripts/backfill_chunks.py` runs but must not be run against production.~~ **Deleted 2026-09-18 (DBT-24, #79)** together with `tests/integration/test_backfill_pipeline.py` and the two unit tests that mirrored its inlined helpers: its fetch query joined on `chunks.article_id` (never written by the live pipeline, so it selected every article — 11,961), it chunked post-SYS-18 prose as one paragraph, and it embedded into a `VECTOR_DB_DIR` index nothing reads; a rewrite meeting the ticket's (a)/(b)/(c) would have been `backfill_orphan_chunks.py` again. **The only chunk backfill tool is `scripts/backfill_orphan_chunks.py`** (keyed on `sitemap_id` per level, prose split on `\n\n`, through `ChunkFAISSDatasource` into `NEWS_L1/L2`), now covered by `tests/integration/test_backfill_orphan_chunks_live.py` — production-format seed, no-op on an already-chunked article, level-aware selection (the DBT-19 survivor), vectors found by `search_l2()`. **A script whose only test seeds its own data format has not been tested against production** — run `--dry-run` on `newsaggregator` and check the count is plausible before trusting it. → DBT-24 (#79) · [2026-09-18 fix](#2026-09-18--sys-29-make-the-sitemap-dedup-key-the-link-alone-and-enforce-it)
- **Nothing in the pipeline is scheduled except the 05:30 health check.** OPERATIONS.md's 06:00/18:00 crawl is a recommendation; `Get-ScheduledTask` shows one job. A closing comment that relies on "the next scheduled run" is relying on a manual run nobody has planned — the last full-list `task=all` is 2026-06-02. → same entry
- ~~`/api/health` costs ~2 s and ~300 MB per call since OPS-04~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (OPS-14, #80):** the FAISS count now comes from `faiss.read_index(index.faiss, IO_FLAG_MMAP).ntotal` (13 ms + 44 ms, no allocation; `ntotal` equals the docstore length LangChain keeps in step) cached on the file's `(mtime, size)` — 0.2 ms warm — and the whole payload runs via `run_in_threadpool`. Endpoint p50 96.8 ms on production data, of which ~95 ms is `get_stats()`'s Postgres COUNTs. **To count a FAISS store, read the index header, never the docstore pickle** — `len(docstore._dict)` materialises every document to answer a question the header already answers. ~~`/api/query` and the other routes still do their blocking work inline (DBT-15, #50, open).~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (DBT-15, #50):** `api_query`/`api_query_form`/`api_stats` now dispatch through `run_in_threadpool` (same pattern), and `_get_processor`/`_get_stats_ds` use double-checked locking around a shared `threading.Lock` so two concurrent first requests can't each construct their own `ChunkFAISSDatasource`. Live-verified against production: 3 concurrent `/api/stats`+`/api/health` requests all completed in ~0.1-0.13s each, ~0.2s total wall time (not serialized); a real end-to-end `/api/query` through the new dispatch path returned in 18s (Gemini-bound) with no wiring errors. → same entry · [2026-09-18 fix](#2026-09-18--sys-29-make-the-sitemap-dedup-key-the-link-alone-and-enforce-it) · [2026-09-19 DBT-15 fix](#2026-09-19--dbt-15-fix-do-the-threadpool-dispatch--singleton-lock-actually-stop-the-event-loop-from-stalling)
- ~~Mutation-testing a "loops forever" fix needs `pytest-timeout` (not in the venv)~~ **Fixed 2026-09-18 (DBT-25, #82):** `pytest-timeout==2.4.0` is installed and pinned, and `pytest.ini` sets `timeout = 120` / `timeout_method = thread` (the only method on Windows — on expiry it dumps every stack and terminates the whole pytest process, exit 1, so a hang is always loud). SYS-28's own mutant no longer exists: the script it lived in was deleted under DBT-24. The other survivor — DBT-20's `AND sitemap_id = ANY(:ids)` — is now caught by `tests/integration/test_fix_rejected_filter_live.py` (two `rejected_filter` rows, one in the L1 set, exactly one returned). **Asserting that a param was *passed* is not asserting that the SQL *uses* it.** → DBT-25 (#82) · same entry
- ~~The semantic filter does not keep "only finance/economy articles" — at 0.35 it saves fashion, gossip, recipes, politics and regional news.~~ **Fixed 2026-09-19 (SYS-31, #86):** `is_offtopic_category` (`core/importance_filter.py`) hard-rejects a known non-finance `sitemaps.category` (lifestyle/hiburan/olahraga/religi/travel/kuliner verticals, all of GRIDID's magazine brands, JPNN's URL-shaped categories via keyword match) before the semantic score ever runs, and `SemanticImportanceFilter` now also takes `offtopic_interests` + a `margin` (0.035, tuned on a 2,500-row sample) as a text-only fallback for articles with no usable category. On a fresh 1,500-row weak-labeled sample: precision went from **0.676** (436 finance-category / 645 labeled) to **1.000** (418/418) — 209/209 negative-category rows now rejected, 418/436 (95.9%) positive-category rows retained. All six titles quoted in the original finding are rejected with their real category. Existing off-topic rows already saved are untouched pending a decision (see ticket) — this only fixes what gets saved going forward. → [2026-09-19 SYS-31 fix](#2026-09-19--sys-31-fix-does-a-category-deny-list--offtopic-anchor-margin-actually-fix-filter-precision) · DBT-04 (#23) still describes the old (wrong) behavior as already handled
- **OPS-15's "source-wide failure" guard is a pure ratio with no floor and stamps nothing.** N/N failing for any N ⇒ nothing counted, no cooldown, no `crawl_log` trace — a queue drained to a few dead URLs (BISNIS: 3) is re-fetched every run forever, and a dead newest-1,000 window stalls a 10k queue. When `fetch_failures` stays 0 for a source that keeps producing 0 articles, that is this, not health. → OPS-16 (#87) · same entry
- **`recrawl_faiss_orphans.py --write-off-remaining` deletes L1 vectors for any row with `fetch_failures > 0`** (one transient timeout), and the script has no tests. Dry-run and read `unrecoverable()` before running it after a flaky crawl. → DBT-27 (#88) · same entry
- **Do not tune the cosine-vs-`INTERESTS`-phrases score again (threshold, phrase list, margin, sentence-length anchors).** Three passes on that signal (0.35 threshold; SYS-31 deny-list; SYS-31 margin) were each found wanting by the next audit -- it rejects world oil prices at 0.254 and passes a slaughtered cow at 0.421 on the *same* scale, and 53% of the corpus sits in [0.35, 0.45). Settled 2026-09-20 (product decision on SYS-35 #118): the scoring step is replaced by a supervised linear probe on the same MiniLM embedding (SYS-37, #121), trained on a Gemini-labelled, reject-inclusive gold set with a written rubric (SYS-36, #120). **A runtime LLM classifier was considered and rejected** -- not on cost (~10M tokens/month, single-digit dollars) but because `rejected_filter` is terminal and `process_event` swallows exceptions, so an API failure in the per-article path must degrade to a local decision anyway, and `tests/unit` is offline by design. The LLM labels (SYS-36) and judges weekly samples (OPS-18, #123); it does not gate. Backlog re-queue with the probe is SYS-38 (#122).
- **The product scope is "news-worthy", not "finance/economy" -- owner decision 2026-09-20, later the same day.** Hard news in (politics, law/crime, disasters, international, economic policy); soft content out (recipes, `doa`/prayer guides, horoskop/zodiak, celebrity gossip, listicles, tips, fiction/poetry, **sports**). The probe is multi-class and writes `articles.topic`; the five finance topics are what the newsletter renders first and extraction prioritises. Consequences, each ticketed: SRC-34 (#124) widens the nine finance-narrowed crawlers **now** (owner overruled the wait-a-week recommendation; `task=sitemap` only until SYS-37 lands, or every general URL is permanently `rejected_filter`), SYS-39 (#125) extraction capacity (the 1,400/day cap is a free-tier artefact from PRD §7.6, not a Gemini limit), SYS-40 (#126) newsletter sectioned by topic + PRD rewrite, SYS-41 (#127) vector-store capacity (IndexFlatL2 in RAM, ~1.5 GB/month at 5k/day). SRC-30, SRC-31 and DBT-04 closed as superseded; SRC-33 re-scoped. **Do not narrow a crawler to a finance leaf again**, and do not read the SRC-05..28 "keep bisnis only" commits as current policy.
- ~~TRIBUN never saves any article: reporter=None crashes NewsDetailsModel~~ **Fixed 2026-09-17 (SRC-09, #57):** `TribunCrawler._get_reporter_from_text` now reads `<div id="penulis">` -- note **id**, not class; a class-based search (`find_all(class_=re.compile(...))`) finds nothing here and looks like "no byline exists" when it's really "wrong search". Also confirmed TRIBUN had 0 sitemap rows despite being on the `HEALTHY` list the whole 2026-09-16/17 remediation window -- `check_crawlers.py`'s "OK" proves extraction works, not that anything is actually being crawled; check row counts too. → [2026-09-17 fix](#2026-09-17--src-09-tribuns-missing-byline-extractor-the-last-p1-fix)
