### 2026-09-20 — full `task=all` end to end for the 8 newly-fixed outlets: 7 clean, 1 real new bug (INEWS)
- **Question / hypothesis:** the previous entry verified `task=sitemap` for TIRTO/SUARA only and `check_crawlers.py` (extraction-only) for the rest. At the user's request, run the actual full pipeline -- sitemap + full-text + semantic filter + chunk + embed + save -- for all 8 outlets from the 2026-09-19/20 batch (ANTARANEWS, TIRTO, SUARA, LIPUTAN6, MERDEKA, TEMPO, IDNTIMES, INEWS) and see what a real run does that a sample can't show.
- **Method:** `lambda_handler.process_event({'task': 'all', 'website': '<8 names>'}, None)` against production, in the background (~15 min); before/after row counts per outlet; grepped the ~5,300-line log for batch summaries, WARNING/ERROR/Traceback; for the one anomaly (INEWS), read `_get_branches`, queried `sitemaps.category`/`fetch_failures` distributions, and live-`requests.get()`'d a sampled dead URL with and without `?page=all` to isolate the cause.
- **Result:**
  - **7/8 outlets: clean, proportionate, matches every commit's claims.** ANTARANEWS 79 sitemaps/45 articles, TIRTO 286(already saved)/191, SUARA 200(already saved)/106, LIPUTAN6 200/89 (extractor=`site`, matching its `OK` status — the only one of the 8 with a live selector), MERDEKA 100/56, TEMPO 200/126, all via `trafilatura` fallback as their commits said. Pass-through rates (45-65%) look like a healthy finance-focused feed.
  - **IDNTIMES: 1,426 sitemaps (all 16 sections, not just business), 99/~930 articles saved (~10.6% pass rate).** Correct outcome (the filter rejects the other 89%, nothing bad gets saved) but disproportionate work for a source whose siblings this session all narrowed to one finance branch. Filed SRC-28 (#110, P3) — a scope-consistency/efficiency question, not a correctness bug.
  - **INEWS: 24,861 new sitemaps in one run — the standout finding.** `_get_branches` (untouched by this session, not part of any ticket) has no filter at all: 126 categories, 90% (22,406 rows) from 113 regional subdomains (`bogor.inews.id`, etc.). Full-text sampled 1,000 newest and got **981 HTTP 404s** — confirmed live on a sampled URL, 404 with and without `?page=all`, so it's dead content, not a suffix bug. Only 1 article saved from the whole run. `SITEMAP_SOURCE_FAILURE_RATIO`'s 100% threshold didn't trip (98.1% < 100%), so OPS-16 (#87)'s predicted "dead top-1000 window, re-fetched forever" shape is now observed live, not just theoretical. Root cause is dormant, pre-existing code that DBT-28 (#98)'s certifi fix incidentally exposed by making INEWS reachable at all — DBT-28 itself is correctly scoped and not at fault. Filed SRC-27 (#109, P2).
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **A source whose crawler code wasn't touched by the current ticket batch is not automatically safe** — DBT-28 only fixed TLS, but fixing the *reachability* of a source can be exactly what turns a long-dormant `_get_branches` bug from invisible into a 24,861-row production incident. When a fix removes a hard blocker (TLS, a 403, a dead URL) that has been masking a source for a long time, check what the *unblocked* code path actually does before assuming the ticket's narrow fix is the end of the story. (2) **Every sibling fix this session that deliberately narrowed to one finance branch (SUARA/MERDEKA/LIPUTAN6/TIRTO/TEMPO) got that right; the two that didn't narrow (IDNTIMES, and INEWS's pre-existing code) are exactly the two with disproportionate/broken output.** Branch-scope narrowing isn't cosmetic — it's load-bearing for a finance-only pipeline. (3) A single aggregate WARNING line (`INEWS: 981/1000 sitemaps failed...`) in a 5,300-line log is easy to miss without grepping for it specifically — a real run's log needs the same "grep for WARNING/ERROR, don't just read the tail" treatment as any other verification.
- **Links:** SRC-27 (#109, P2) filed · SRC-28 (#110, P3) filed · real production writes this run: ANTARANEWS +79 sitemaps/+45 articles, TIRTO +191 articles, SUARA +106 articles, LIPUTAN6 +200 sitemaps/+89 articles, MERDEKA +100 sitemaps/+56 articles, TEMPO +200 sitemaps/+126 articles, IDNTIMES +1,426 sitemaps/+99 articles, INEWS +24,861 sitemaps/+1 article — all left in place as real data, not rolled back; INEWS's regional-subdomain rows are not yet cleaned up (see SRC-27's done-when) · related: OPS-16 (#87)

### 2026-09-20 — fixing the 4 tickets filed by the remediation-batch QA audit (SRC-27, SRC-28, DBT-29, DBT-30)
- **Question / hypothesis:** does narrowing `_get_branches` to the finance-relevant leaf (the pattern every sibling fix this session already used) actually cut INEWS/IDNTIMES back down to a proportionate row count, without another live `_get_branches`-ignores-`soup`-style surprise?
- **Method:** fetched INEWS's real index live (`INewsCrawler().page_loader.get_soup('https://www.inews.id/sitemap.xml')`) to find the actual finance leaf's URL shape before writing the filter, rather than guessing from the category label alone — confirmed `https://www.inews.id/finance/bisnis/sitemap.xml` is the one `www.inews.id` entry under a `finance` path (the other 128 branches are other topical sections or one of 113 dead regional subdomains). Rewrote `INewsCrawler._get_branches` to keep only that leaf (endswith match, same pattern as SUARA/MERDEKA/TEMPO's fixes) and `IDNTimesCrawler._get_branches` to additionally require `business` as the matched section (SRC-14's regex already isolated `/sitemap-news.xml` entries; this just narrows the accepted section down from all 16). For DBT-29, added `sitemaps.category` to `backfill_sitemap_crawl_status.py`'s `_FETCH_SQL` and gated `classify_batch`'s non-blank-headline set through `is_offtopic_category` before any encoding happens, mirroring `SemanticImportanceFilter.is_important`'s own category-before-score ordering. For DBT-30, one-line typo fix. Verified with `tests/unit` (new `test_inews_crawler.py`, `test_backfill_sitemap_crawl_status.py`, updated `test_idntimes_crawler.py`) and a live `check_crawlers.py --website INEWS,IDNTIMES --sample-size 2` run (no DB writes).
- **Result:** `check_crawlers.py` shows both outlets now scraping exactly one branch each (`finance` / `business`), still `FALLBACK` as already separately ticketed (SRC-19, SRC-22) — unrelated to this fix. Full unit suite: 552 passed (up from 543 pre-session + this batch's 9 new/updated). Did not re-run a live `task=sitemap`/`task=all` for INEWS/IDNTIMES to confirm the row-count drop at scale (that would add more real production rows on top of the 24,861 + 1,426 already sitting in `sitemaps` from the previous entry's verification run) — left for the user to trigger if they want end-to-end confirmation before closing.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** the already-ingested 24,861 INEWS / 1,426 IDNTIMES sitemap rows from the prior verification run are NOT cleaned up by this fix — it only stops the bleeding going forward. Those rows have no `articles` row and will keep retrying under the normal cooldown until `SITEMAP_MAX_FETCH_FAILURES` excludes them (SYS-09) — self-limiting, not filed as a new ticket, but worth knowing before assuming `sitemaps` row counts for these two sources reflect only good data.
- **Links:** SRC-27 (#109), SRC-28 (#110), DBT-29 (#107), DBT-30 (#108) — all fixed this entry, closing pending user confirmation.

### 2026-09-20 — end-to-end task=all for INEWS/IDNTIMES after the SRC-27/SRC-28 fixes: is the "self-limiting" backlog claim actually true?
- **Question / hypothesis:** the previous entry's conclusion assumed the 24,861 INEWS / 1,426 IDNTIMES pre-existing junk sitemap rows were "self-limiting" via SYS-09's fetch-failure cooldown. At the user's request, run a real `task=all` for both outlets and check.
- **Method:** `lambda_handler.process_event({'task': 'all', 'website': 'INEWS,IDNTIMES'}, None)` in the background. First attempt was killed by the harness partway through IDNTIMES (system-wide low memory, ~6GB free of 32GB at the time -- not a code bug); INEWS's run had already completed in full before the kill, so its numbers are complete. Re-ran `task=all` for IDNTIMES alone to finish that half. Cross-checked both against `sitemaps`/`articles` row counts and `fetch_failures` distribution before/after.
- **Result:**
  - **INEWS: 995/1000 sitemaps failed again (99.5%, all HTTP 404) -- essentially identical to the pre-fix run's 981/1000.** SRC-27's `_get_branches` fix only stops *new* sitemap rows from non-finance branches; `load_all_sitemaps` has no category awareness and pulled from the pre-existing 22,406-row dead-regional-subdomain backlog exactly as before. `fetch_failures` distribution: (0, 22885), (1, 1976) -- 1976 = 981 + 995, confirming two disjoint ~1,000-row windows, both near-total 404. 22,861 rows remain eligible (only 198 of them `finance`). At `SITEMAP_MAX_FETCH_FAILURES=3` and a 7-day cooldown, and with no scheduled `task=all`/`full_text` job, this backlog will not clear on any predictable timeline -- filed **SRC-29 (#111, P2)**.
  - **INEWS's finance branch itself added 0 new rows this run** (`get 200 to scrape for INEWS`, all 200 already known) -- the site's finance sitemap window hasn't rotated since the prior investigative crawl; not a bug, just means this run couldn't demonstrate finance-branch growth the way IDNTIMES's did.
  - **IDNTIMES: clean, no warnings.** `attempted=399 extracted=370 passed_filter=46 saved=46` (~11.5% pass rate, matching the ~10.6% seen in the full 8-outlet run). `business` category sitemaps grew 130 -> 147 (17 new, real finance content from the live site) confirming SRC-28's narrowed branch is actively working going forward. Articles still came from a mix of all 14 categories (business 83, news 46, life 8, ...) since `load_all_sitemaps` pulls the pre-existing backlog same as INEWS -- but since IDNTIMES's off-branch content isn't *dead* (just off-topic), this is recurring compute waste, not wasted network 404s, and was already captured by SRC-28's own text.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** **"the backlog is self-limiting" was an underestimate — check the actual math (attempts needed x cooldown days x whether a recurring job even exists) before calling a stale-data problem self-resolving.** A `_get_branches` fix that narrows future sitemap discovery does nothing about a category-blind `load_all_sitemaps` continuing to serve the pre-existing backlog; for a source whose backlog is genuinely dead (not just off-topic), that means real, repeated, measurable waste (near-1,000 wasted HTTP requests) on every future ad hoc run, not a one-time cost.
- **Links:** SRC-29 (#111, P2) filed, open · real production writes this run: INEWS +1 article (now 2 total), IDNTIMES +46 articles/+17 sitemaps · related: SRC-27 (#109), OPS-16 (#87), DBT-04 (#23)

### 2026-09-20 — is SRC-29's dead-backlog problem INEWS-specific, or do other sources have it too?
- **Question / hypothesis:** the user asked whether SRC-29's shape (a confirmed-dead sitemap backlog silently burning ~1,000 wasted requests per run, because `load_all_sitemaps` has no category/branch awareness and OPS-16's ratio guard never trips below 100%) is unique to INEWS or affects other sources too. Answered by querying the real DB, not by reasoning from the code alone.
- **Method:**
  ```sql
  SELECT sm.sources,
         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.sitemap_id IS NULL) AS no_article,
         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.sitemap_id IS NULL AND sm.crawl_status != 'rejected_filter'
                           AND (sm.last_crawl_attempt IS NULL OR sm.last_crawl_attempt < NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days')
                           AND sm.fetch_failures < 3) AS eligible_now,
         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE sm.fetch_failures > 0) AS ever_failed,
         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE sm.fetch_failures >= 3) AS excluded_dead,
         COUNT(*) AS total
  FROM sitemaps sm LEFT JOIN articles a ON a.sitemap_id = sm.sitemap_id
  GROUP BY sm.sources ORDER BY eligible_now DESC;
  ```
  Cross-referenced the largest queues against their own tracking history: SYS-08 (#26, VIVA) and OPS-16 (#87, BISNIS)'s existing text.
- **Result:**
  - **The 7 sibling outlets fixed this session (ANTARANEWS, TIRTO, SUARA, LIPUTAN6, MERDEKA, TEMPO, IDNTIMES): not affected.** `ever_failed=0` for all of them except IDNTIMES (93, ordinary per-URL extraction misses from today's own run, not a systemic dead-domain shape) -- their real `task=all` runs today extracted content fine; the remaining no-article backlog is small (34-1,285 rows) and off-topic-but-alive, not dead.
  - **BISNIS already has the identical shape, at small scale (3-row eligible queue, 403-blocked)** -- this is the worked example already cited in OPS-16 (#87)'s own text, not a new finding.
  - **VIVA has by far the largest eligible queue in the pipeline (39,211 rows, `ever_failed=0`) but it is NOT the same problem** -- SYS-08 (#26, closed) root-caused VIVA's zero-article history to a pydantic validation bug on `reporter` (fixed in `e9018ed`), not dead URLs. That ~39K backlog was deliberately never re-crawled after the fix ("not now, later"), so whether it's now healthy or hides a different dead-URL problem is untested, not confirmed either way.
  - **MEDIAINDONESIA (10,960 eligible), PIKIRANRAKYAT (3,560), JPNN (2,538), WARTAEKONOMI (1,774), CNBC (1,345), IDXCHANNEL (1,074), TVONENEWS (807), SINDONEWS (632), GRIDID (434), KUMPARAN (352), KOMPAS (223), CNN (154): unknown, not tested.** All show `ever_failed=0`, which only proves nothing has hit the per-row failure counter yet -- consistent with either "genuinely healthy backlog" or "hasn't been crawled with `task=all`/`full_text` since the counter went live." No live evidence either way; not spot-checked further per the user's call ("no need... I'll let the QA team audit that").
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** SRC-29's specific symptom (measured 99%+ HTTP-404 burn rate) is INEWS-only among sources actually tested. The *architectural* gap it exposed (category-blind queue selection + a ratio guard that can't fire below 100%) is generic and already has one other live instance (BISNIS, small scale, covered by OPS-16) -- but "the gap is generic" is not evidence that any specific large-queue source (VIVA, MEDIAINDONESIA, etc.) currently has a dead backlog. Don't infer that from queue size alone; `ever_failed=0` on an untested source is not the same claim as `ever_failed=0` on a source verified healthy today (INEWS's own `ever_failed` was 0 too, right up until the first real run that measured it).
- **Links:** no new ticket filed (informational cross-check only, at the user's explicit direction) · related: SRC-29 (#111), OPS-16 (#87), SYS-08 (#26, closed)

### 2026-09-20 — QA audit of the SRC-27/SRC-28/DBT-29/DBT-30 closures, with a fresh-eyes pass over the whole 2026-09-19/20 batch: 6 new tickets
- **Question / hypothesis:** the four tickets filed by the previous QA pass were fixed and closed "pending user confirmation" by the same session, with no independent audit. Do they hold? And does the rest of the batch (SRC-05/12/13/15/16, SYS-31, SYS-32, DBT-10/15/28) still hold when checked against the *live* sites and the *live* DB rather than the fixtures the fixes were written against?
- **Method:** full diff read of `e11eecd`, `87193a5`, `437aa82`, `6f76119` and the current state of every scraper/filter file the batch touched; `tests/unit` (552 passed) and `tests/integration` (102 passed) run whole; `scripts/check_store_consistency.py` (read-only) to confirm SYS-31's SQL+FAISS delete left the stores in agreement; `crawl_log` checked for the 2026-09-20 05:30 WIB scheduled health check as independent evidence; then, instead of re-running mutation tests on guards that are trivially strong (each of the three new tests fails on a direct revert), every branch-narrowing fix was re-checked against its outlet's **live index today** (`_get_branches(soup)` vs. every `<sitemap>` entry that looks finance-related), SYS-32's headline fallback against the **live page** (`og:title` vs. `_derive_headline_from_url`), SYS-31's deny-list against the **categories actually saved** from the eight newly-fixed outlets, and DBT-29's `classify_batch` against the live `is_important` decision over the real backfill candidate set (12,472 non-VIVA rows + 4,000 sampled VIVA rows, batch-encoded, headline-only on both sides). Last, the question the previous session left to QA -- does SRC-29's dead-backlog shape exist on the untested large queues -- was answered with a sampled liveness probe (25 random eligible-backlog URLs per source, 13 sources, 8 threads, `requests.get` status only). Probe scripts were throwaway (inlined logic described per finding); nothing written to the DB.
- **Result:**
  - **The four closures do what they say.** SRC-27: `_get_branches` returns exactly `{finance: .../finance/bisnis/sitemap.xml}` on the live index; SRC-28: exactly `{business: ...}`; DBT-29: category-denied rows never reach the model; DBT-30: typo fixed. Store consistency 2/9 nonzero, both `KNOWN` (VIVA debris at baseline), 0 FAISS orphans after the 1,593-row cleanup. Scheduled health check 2026-09-19 22:30 UTC: all 8 fixed outlets `OK`/`FALLBACK`, KAPANLAGI/BATAMPOS absent, BISNIS still `BROKEN`. No dangling references to the retired crawlers outside comments and the abandoned `services/` tree.
  - **But SRC-27 over-narrowed on a false premise -- SRC-30 (#112, P2).** The commit says `finance/bisnis` is "the one `www.inews.id` entry under a `finance` path". The live index (177 entries today, not 129) has four: `finance/`, `finance/keuangan/`, `finance/makro/`, `finance/bisnis/`. Each sub-section is a 200-URL Google-news sitemap; the three are **pairwise disjoint** (union 600) and `finance/` is a strict subset of the union. `keuangan` ("Jelang Akhir Pekan, IHSG Ditutup Loyo ke Level 6.441") and `makro` ("Pemerintah Tarik Utang Rp506 Triliun", "Cadangan Devisa RI Naik jadi 146,5 Miliar Dolar AS") are closer to `INTERESTS` than `bisnis` is. Two thirds of INEWS's finance output is silently dropped.
  - **Same shape on LIPUTAN6 -- SRC-31 (#113, P2).** SRC-05 keeps `bisnis` only; the live index also has `saham/sitemap.xml` -- 200 URLs, overlap with `bisnis` = 0, all capital-markets ("Edwin Soeryadjaya Beli 1,24 Juta Saham SRTG", "Emiten SMDR Menambah Modal"). The kept `bisnis` leaf meanwhile carries "Riset: 73% Gen Z Takut Meminta Bantuan saat Bangun Relasi". SUARA (`bisnis` only), MERDEKA (`uang` only), TEMPO (`ekonomi`; `investigasi` is not finance), TIRTO (`ekonomi`+`bisnis`) and IDNTIMES (`business` is the only finance leaf of 17) were checked the same way and are correctly scoped.
  - **SYS-32's placeholder headline is permanent -- SYS-33 (#114, P2).** All 317 TIRTO/TEMPO articles have `sitemaps.headline` = `str.title()`-cased URL slug with the trailing id: `"Rupiah Dan Ihsg Menguat 9 September 2026 Ini Pergerakannya Hca5"`, `"Apa Dampak Kelangkaan Bbm Bersubsidi 2287141"`. Both pages carry a clean `og:title` and `Crawler._get_page_title(soup)` returns it -- but `_fetch_news_details` only calls it when `sitemap.headline` is *empty*, which `_get_sitemap`'s own fallback guarantees never happens on the live path (the code comment admits this). Downstream, everything -- filter input, `chunks.title`, FAISS metadata, RAG UI, newsletter, knowledge extraction -- reads `sitemaps.headline`, so the placeholder is the row's identity forever.
  - **SYS-31 regressed on the first new multi-section source -- SYS-34 (#115, P2).** IDNTIMES's 2026-09-20 full-section run (pre-SRC-28) saved 158 articles; 29 (18.4%) are under `fiction`/`hype`/`korea`/`life`/`men`/`science`/`health`/`food`, and at least 26 are exactly SYS-31's complaint: `[PUISI] Barangkali Aku Butuh Jeda`, `Harry Vaughan Umumkan Pernikahan dengan Nevy Tania`, `Krisjiana, Suami Siti Badriah, Jalani Vasektomi`, `3 Profesi Dilakoni Han So Hee di Film`, `7 Gaya OOTD Dewi Gita`, `5 Zodiak yang Punya Aura Pembawa Rezeki`. The deny-list has `lifestyle`/`hiburan`/`kuliner` but not `life`/`hype`/`food`; it is allow-by-default and was built from the vocabulary in the DB on 09-19. The margin fallback -- the mechanism meant to catch what the deny-list misses -- passed a poem. SYS-31's "precision 1.000" was measured on the distribution the deny-list was built from. None of the 29 are entity-referenced; `cleanup_offtopic_articles.py` will remove them once the list is extended. 93 IDNTIMES backlog rows in those categories remain eligible for re-crawl.
  - **DBT-29 ported half of SYS-31 -- DBT-31 (#116, P3).** The backfill builds its filter without `offtopic_interests` and `classify_batch` never computes the margin, yet its docstring now says "Same decision as `is_important()`". Measured: of **10,241** candidate rows `classify_batch` would leave `pending`, the live decision rejects **3,676 (35.9%)** via the margin (`donwori` -> "Cinta Kandas Karena Sakit Saraf" 0.364/0.410; `teknologi` -> "VNGGames Siap Rilis Game Sepak Bola" 0.353/0.586). VIVA was sampled at 4,000 of 37,330, so the full-queue number is roughly 2x that.
  - **TIRTO's newest-leaf policy loses every month's tail -- SRC-32 (#117, P3).** `insider/ekonomi.xml` lists 27 monthly leaves; `_newest_monthly_leaf` returns one. August's leaf has `lastmod 2026-08-31T19:10+07:00`; from Sept 1 it is never fetched again, so anything published between the last August crawl and month-end is never listed. With nothing scheduled, that gap is days.
  - **SRC-29's dead-backlog shape is INEWS-only -- no ticket.** 25 random eligible-backlog URLs per source: VIVA 25/25 `200`, MEDIAINDONESIA 25/25, JPNN 25/25, WARTAEKONOMI 25/25, CNBC 25/25, IDXCHANNEL 25/25, KUMPARAN 25/25, KOMPAS 25/25, CNN 25/25, TVONENEWS 24 + one `502`, GRIDID 24 + one `410`, PIKIRANRAKYAT 19 + 6 `ConnectionError` (rate-limited under 8 threads, not dead), SINDONEWS 20 + 5 `ReadTimeout` (slow site). Status-only, so a soft-404 would not show here -- but SYS-14's soft-404 check is in the pipeline for that.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **"Narrow `_get_branches` to the finance leaf" is only right if you first list every finance-looking `<sitemap>` in the live index and decide about each one.** Two of the seven sibling fixes matched a single literal leaf and dropped disjoint finance sections (INEWS `keuangan`/`makro`, LIPUTAN6 `saham`); the commit for one of them asserted a fact about the index that was false on the day it was checked. A one-line probe -- every `<loc>` matching `ekonomi|bisnis|finan|keuangan|uang|saham|bursa|invest|makro|business|econ` -- against `_get_branches(soup)` is the check; run it before closing any SRC branch ticket. (2) **A fallback that exists in the code is not a fallback that runs** -- SYS-32 wired `_get_page_title` behind a condition its own sibling code makes unreachable. When a fix adds "prefer X over Y when Y is empty", check whether Y can ever be empty on the live path. (3) **A deny-list built from the current corpus has zero coverage of the next source's vocabulary by construction.** Measuring precision on that same corpus cannot detect this. Any allow-by-default filter needs a "first time this category value produced a saved article" signal or it will be found by the next audit, not the next run. (4) **A ticket whose Symptom names two mechanisms and whose Done-when names one will be fixed to the Done-when** -- DBT-29's fix is exactly its Done-when and exactly half its Symptom. When filing, put every mechanism in Done-when. (5) The previous session's "let QA audit whether other backlogs are dead" was a 4-minute sampled probe, not a hard question -- do the cheap measurement instead of leaving the inference open.
- **Links:** SRC-30 (#112), SRC-31 (#113), SYS-33 (#114), SYS-34 (#115), DBT-31 (#116), SRC-32 (#117) filed · verified held: SRC-27 (#109), SRC-28 (#110), DBT-29 (#107, as scoped), DBT-30 (#108), SRC-12 (#90), SRC-13 (#91), SRC-14 (#92), SRC-15 (#93), SRC-16 (#95, as scoped), SYS-32 (#94, as scoped), DBT-10 (#45), DBT-15 (#50), DBT-28 (#98), SRC-02 (#13) · no production writes this session

### 2026-09-20 — full audit of VIVA: what is actually in the 39k backlog, what a real run would save, and why the finance section itself loses 42%
- **Question / hypothesis:** VIVA is the largest queue in the pipeline (37k+ eligible rows, `ever_failed=0`, never crawled end to end since SYS-08 fixed why it saved nothing). The liveness probe earlier today showed the URLs are alive; the user asked for the full audit. Is the backlog worth crawling, what would the current pipeline do with it, and is the source itself healthy?
- **Method:** read-only throughout -- **no `save_*` call and no DB write**. (1) DB profile: status/age/category distribution of all 39,261 VIVA rows, plus the exact composition of `load_all_sitemaps`' newest-1,000 window. (2) `viva_crawler.py` + `git log`. (3) Live index: every `<sitemap>` entry vs `_get_branches(soup)`; `bisnis.xml` leaf inspected. (4) `is_offtopic_category` over all 52 category values with 4 random titles each (the SYS-31/SYS-34 sample-titles-before-trusting-a-name rule). (5) Headline-only simulation of the full live decision (category gate + threshold + margin) over all 39,261 rows, batch-encoded, 165 s. (6) Dry run through the *real* code: 100 rows sampled from the newest-1,000 window and 100 from `bisnis` built into `SitemapDTO`s, fetched with `VivaCrawler.batch_crawling_details` (real page loader, real extractor chain), scored with the service's own `check_text = f"{title} {body[:500]}"` and `category` through a `SemanticImportanceFilter` built exactly as `CrawlerServiceImpl` builds it. (7) 150 more `bisnis` rows the same way, attributing each rejection to the threshold or the margin step. (8) Because (7) pointed at the threshold, `SELECT rejected_filter vs saved` under finance-section categories for every source, a 36-row sample of this week's finance-section rejects, and 8 of those re-fetched and re-scored with their real bodies.
- **Result:**
  - **Backlog:** 39,261 rows, 50 articles (all from SYS-08's 2026-09-16 verification), 39,211 eligible now, spanning 2008 (`breaking-news`) to 2026-06-02 -- **no sitemap crawl since June 2**, so 3.5 months of `bisnis` never listed. `bisnis` = **863 rows (2.2%)**. 24 `www` sections + **29 regional/vertical subdomains stored with their full URL as `category`** (24,000 rows; `re.sub` returns the unmatched link unchanged -- SRC-14's IDNTIMES bug). 9,447 rows carry June-2026 chunks/FAISS but no article (the OPS-04 `KNOWN` debris). The newest-1,000 window a `task=full_text` would fetch today: 525 subdomain + 474 `news-sitemap` + 1 `gaya-hidup`, **0 `bisnis`**.
  - **Live index:** 82 entries; `_get_branches` returns **53 branches** (up to 1,000 URLs each) -- SRC-27's INEWS shape, untouched. `bisnis.xml` is a clean Google-news leaf (1,000 `<url>`, 2026-08-18..09-20) and the only finance section in the index. Site extractor alive: `extractor=site` on 350/350 fetches, 0 fetch failures.
  - **Deny-list on VIVA:** 3,088 / 39,261 denied (7.9%). `showbiz` 914 not denied; `https://wisata.viva.co.id/...` not denied (`wisata` is only in the exact list); `militer`/`pilkada`/`vbuzz`/`trending`/`blog`/`olret`/`gadget`/`techno` no keyword. → comment on SYS-34 (#115).
  - **Headline-only live decision over everything:** keeps 9,840, **461 of them `bisnis`** -- 95% of what the filter would keep is non-finance (`Persib Juara Lagi!`, `Hukum Memakai Masker Ketika Ihram` 0.617, `Gaet Betina, Monyet Jantan Mandi Air Seni` 0.514, `pilkada` 510/1,000, `indepth` 526/1,000).
  - **Dry run, real code, title+body:** window sample 100 → **13 would be saved**, ~6 off-topic (`Sponsor Utama Piala AFF U-19`, `RUU Polri: Tembakan Terukur ke Begal`, `Gaya Nadiem ... Pakai Jaket Gojek`, `Manfaat Lilin Lebah untuk Aromaterapi`), 87 → `rejected_filter`. `bisnis` sample 100 → **62 saved, 38 rejected**, and the rejects are real economy news (`Bahlil Bahas Skema Bagi Hasil Sektor Tambang dan Harga Crude BBM`, `Petrokimia Gresik Teken Perjanjian Jual-Beli Gas`, `Menhub: Tarif Batas Atas Tiket Pesawat Bakal Naik`, `Airlangga: WFH Bisa Tekan Konsumsi BBM hingga 9 Persen`, `13.000 Penerbangan Global Dibatalkan`).
  - **Attribution on 150 `bisnis` rows:** kept 83, **below threshold 63 (42%)**, margin-rejected 4 (3%). The loss is the original 0.35 raw threshold, not SYS-31's margin.
  - **It is every source, not VIVA:** `rejected_filter` / (`rejected_filter` + saved) under finance-section categories -- LIPUTAN6 `bisnis` **55.5%**, SUARA `bisnis` 47.0%, MERDEKA `uang` 44.0%, WARTAEKONOMI `bisnis` 44.4%, IDNTIMES `business` 40.7%, SINDONEWS `ekbis` 39.0%, TEMPO `ekonomi` 37.0%, JPNN `ekonomi` 36.0%, MEDIAINDONESIA `ekonomi` 35.9%, TIRTO `ekonomi` 33.5%, TVONENEWS `ekonomi` 29.5%; vs WARTAEKONOMI `perbankan`/`makro`/`bursa`/`finansial` 6-12% and CNBC `market` 8.6%. ~1,100 rows permanently rejected under finance categories, ~800 of them this week. 36 sampled: ~30 genuine (`Harga Minyak Dunia Turun Usai AS Ungkap Pipa Arab Saudi`, `Produksi Pesawat Terhambat, Saham Boeing Turun Tajam`, `BI Jajaki Kerja Sama QRIS dengan 4 Negara Baru`, `Utang Whoosh Akan Dilimpahkan Ke Kemenkeu`).
  - **Why:** re-fetching 8 of those: `extractor=site`, clean bodies (`"Liputan6.com, Jakarta - Harga minyak dunia turun pada perdagangan Rabu..."`), and the real `check_text` scores **0.254** (best interest `Berita Penggerak Pasar`); Boeing 0.203; BI/QRIS 0.288 (`Kebijakan Bank Sentral`); Whoosh 0.344; DJP/Pertamina 0.332; Pintu crypto 0.322 (`Pasar Saham`); Rusal alumina 0.328. Adding the body *lowered* the score in 6 of 8. The model + one-to-three-word anchors cannot recognise broad economy coverage; it only works on narrowly-financial text.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **Do not run `task=sitemap` or `task=all` for VIVA until SRC-33 lands** -- 53 branches × 1,000 URLs on top of a 39k backlog that is 97.8% off-topic, and the newest-1,000 `full_text` window contains zero finance rows. (2) **"Pass-through rate 45-65% looks like a healthy finance feed" was wrong** -- it was never checked against what was rejected. On a section that is finance by construction, the pass rate *is* the recall, and it is 45-70%; read the rejects, not the rate. (3) **A dry run through the real crawler + real filter, with nothing saved, answers "what would a real run do" without paying for it in production rows** -- ~200 fetches, 30 s, and none of the ~130 junk articles a real 1,000-row run would have written. Use this shape before any first real run on a big untested backlog; `check_crawlers.py` can't (no filter), and a real run can't be undone cleanly (`rejected_filter` is terminal). (4) The category gate is asymmetric by accident, not design: a category known to be off-topic wins before the score; a category known to be finance still has to survive a score that rejects world oil prices. (5) `rejected_filter` as a permanent terminal state (SYS-16) turns every filter false negative into silent, unrecoverable data loss -- any recall fix must come with a reset of the rows it would have kept.
- **Links:** SYS-35 (#118, P1) filed · SRC-33 (#119, P2) filed · evidence comment on SYS-34 (#115) · related: SRC-27 (#109), SRC-14 (#92), SYS-08 (#26), SYS-16 (#37), SYS-31 (#86), OPS-04 (#52), DBT-04 (#23) · no production writes this session

### 2026-09-20 — does the news-worthy scope change reach the scrapers? Live branch sets of all 30 registered crawlers
- **Question / hypothesis:** the owner asked whether the scope change (SYS-36 #120) "is also applied for the scrapers". SRC-34 (#124) widens the nine crawlers that were narrowed to a finance leaf; what do the other twenty list today, and does anything drop soft verticals before the fetch?
- **Method:** `scripts/experiments/probe_branch_scope.py` -- fetches each registered crawler's index once through its own `page_loader` and runs its real `_get_branches(soup)`; counts keys matching a soft-vertical regex and a finance regex, flags URL-shaped keys. 6 threads, ~40 s, read-only. Then `SELECT sources, COUNT(*), COUNT(a.sitemap_id), status counts, MAX(posted_at), MAX(last_crawl_attempt) FROM sitemaps ... GROUP BY 1` for the DB side, and three direct fetches of Kompas indexes.
- **Result:**
  - **14 of the 20 non-narrowed sources list soft verticals at branch level today**: DETIK 66/237 (`sepakbola/*`, `games*`, `gadget`, `fotostop_*`, `esport`), OKEZONE 18/34 (`women.*`, `celebrity.*`, `bola`, `sports`), JPNN 23/90, GRIDID 21/43, KUMPARAN 6/10, SINDONEWS 5/12, CNN 5/9, TVONENEWS 4/7, TRIBUN 4/14, IDXCHANNEL 4/17, BERITASATU 3/19, CNBC 1/10, KONTAN 2/43, PIKIRANRAKYAT 2/127. Nothing drops them; they are listed, queued ahead of news (newest-first, 1,000/run), fetched, and rejected by category if the vocabulary happens to be covered. WARTAEKONOMI's 7 "soft" hits are the regex's false positives (`transportasi` -- the SYS-31 `sport` trap again -- plus `otomotif`/`kesehatan`, which SYS-31 already verified as business coverage there): a global regex is a starting list, not a decision.
  - **CNN keys every branch by its full URL** -- the regex at `cnn_crawler.py:33` wants two path segments, the live index has one; all 9 DB categories are URLs; `gaya-hidup` (exact-list only) is invisible to the deny-list → 5 lifestyle articles saved. Third instance of the SRC-14/SRC-33 `re.sub`-returns-input bug.
  - **KOMPAS has no finance section**: `URL.KOMPAS` is `news.kompas.com/news/sitemap.xml` (10 news/regional subdomains); `money.kompas.com/news/sitemap.xml` (→ `sitemap-news-money.xml`) and `www.kompas.com/sitemap.xml` (51 sections: `tren`, `global`, `properti`, provinces, plus soft `hype`/`food`/`homey`) exist and were never listed. A finance-only pipeline never read Kompas Money.
  - **GRIDID**: 2,165 rows, 0 articles ever, 1,731 rejected; all 43 live branches are Grid Network magazines. Same shape as the KAPANLAGI/BATAMPOS retirements.
  - **Six `HEALTHY` sources have never produced a sitemap row**: BERITASATU, DETIK, EMITENNEWS, ERAID, INVESTORID, KONTAN. OKEZONE: 1,020 rows, `last_crawl_attempt` NULL on all. Nine sources untouched since 2026-06-02 (CNN, GRIDID, JPNN, KOMPAS, KUMPARAN, PIKIRANRAKYAT, SINDONEWS, TVONENEWS, WARTAEKONOMI). Only the eight SRC-fixed outlets ran in September, by hand. DETIK's 10 "completed" `crawl_log` rows are the health check.
  - Not investigated: KUMPARAN 988 rows / 0 articles / 636 rejected (may be all-soft branches + SYS-35 recall; check when SRC-35 touches it).
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **The scope change reaches the scrapers in two halves, and SRC-34 is only one of them**: the nine narrowed crawlers need widening (SRC-34), the other twenty need soft verticals dropped -- and that belongs in one helper in the base `Crawler`, not twenty regexes (SRC-35 #128). (2) **Run `probe_branch_scope.py` before and after any `_get_branches` change**; it is the whole-fleet version of the SRC-30/31 "list every leaf" trap and takes 40 s. (3) **`check_crawlers.py` OK + `HEALTHY` membership says nothing about whether a source has ever been crawled** -- six never were. The per-source row/status table above is the check; it should be in the daily health output (OPS-19 #132). (4) A URL-shaped `sitemaps.category` is now a three-source pattern; the base crawler should refuse to key a branch by a URL rather than each subclass remembering to.
- **Links:** SRC-35 (#128), SRC-36 (#129), SRC-37 (#130), SRC-38 (#131), OPS-19 (#132) filed · SRC-34 (#124) scope comment · `scripts/experiments/probe_branch_scope.py` · no production writes

### 2026-09-20 — Phase 0 of the news-worthy programme: SYS-22, SRC-11, SRC-36/SRC-41, SYS-35/SYS-34, SYS-33, SYS-36
- **Question / hypothesis:** `HANDOFF_2026-09-20.md` Phase 0 -- seven tickets that must land before anything is widened, scaled or scheduled. Each carries an owner-gated production write; the question for each was "what does the real DB/site say the write would do", answered with a `--dry-run` before any of them ran. Along the way: does the CNN URL-key bug exist anywhere else, what does a category *name* actually contain, and does the SYS-33 placeholder test hold on the whole table?
- **Method:** per ticket -- code + tests + mutation check (`tests/unit` 634 passed, `tests/integration` 102 passed at the end); one live probe each (read-only): KUMPARAN's ld+json shape on 3 pages (`.string`, not `get_text()` -- bs4 ≥ 4.9 skips `<script>` contents); CNN's and JPNN's live sitemap index vs `_get_branches` (`scripts/experiments/probe_branch_scope.py --website CNN,JPNN`); the full `(source, category)` vocabulary with saved/rejected counts (466 pairs) and **3-4 random titles for every name that was about to go on the deny-list or the allow-set** (52 pairs sampled); og:title on 2 TIRTO/TEMPO pages, then `repair_placeholder_headlines.py --dry-run` on all 486 (read-only fetches); `label_filter_gold.py sample` over all 129,391 sitemap rows; `label --max-calls 2` as the Gemini smoke test.
- **Result:**
  - **SYS-22:** one-line split fix; `TestGetKeywords` had pinned the bug (`"economy,"` was the expected token). Existing rows left alone -- nothing reads the column and a whitespace-split single phrase has no comma to recover from.
  - **SRC-11:** `eval` replaced by `json.loads` on `script.string`; the three live bylines are identical through both paths. A repo-wide "no `eval(`" test guards it.
  - **SRC-36 + SRC-41 (new, #135):** CNN's live index has 9 one-segment `sitemap_news.xml` leaves, so the two-segment regex never matched and all 682 rows carry a URL category. **JPNN has the same bug live**: 78 of 90 branches are regional-subdomain URLs (`bali.jpnn.com/sitemap_post_politik.xml`), 6,104 stored rows -- the 09-20 fleet probe printed `URL-KEYS` for it and only CNN got a ticket. Both crawlers now expose `branch_name_from_url()`; JPNN's key is `<region>/<section>` because fifteen `politik` leaves would otherwise collapse to one dict entry. `fix_url_shaped_categories.py --dry-run`: CNN 682 → 9 names, JPNN 6,104 → 63 names, 0 unmappable. After the fix both probe with no `URL-KEYS`, JPNN still 90 branches. Health: CNN `FALLBACK` (pre-existing), JPNN `OK`.
  - **SYS-35 + SYS-34:** the vocabulary sampling changed the lists in both directions. Off the deny-list: `kriminal` (per the owner) and `edukasi` (3-of-4 education *news* on SINDONEWS/OKEZONE/VIVA: Kemendikdasmen rules, PDSS schools, a new UI rector). Onto it beyond the ticket's six: OKEZONE `celebrity`/`women`, KUMPARAN `woman`/`mom`/`bolanita`, TRIBUN `seleb`/`musik`/`superskor`, JPNN `donwori` (a relationship-confession column, **24 saved**)/`advetorial`, VIVA `showbiz`/`siaran-pers`/`vstory`/`blog`/`english`, MEDIAINDONESIA `sajak-kofe`, GRIDID `SuperBall`/`Bobo`/`Otoseken`. Looked soft, sampled as news, kept off: OKEZONE `muslim` (haji security, Wamenhaj), `ototekno` (Meta subscriptions, an AI presenter), IDXCHANNEL `foto`/`video` (finance posts with bodies). Allow-set: the ticket's finance names plus WARTAEKONOMI's sub-desks (`energi`, `industri`, `asuransi`, `pajak`, `properti`, `infrastruktur`, `digital_economy`, ...) and the hard-news desks; **`news`/`berita`/`news-sitemap` left off** -- the owner's comment lists `news` but also says "on `news`/`berita` the category alone does not say news-worthy", and KUMPARAN `news` sampled 1-of-4 hard news (a photo gallery, a how-to and an opinion piece beside it), VIVA `news-sitemap` 0-of-4. `requeue_finance_rejects.py --dry-run`: **2,080** finance-section `rejected_filter` rows (the ticket's ~1,100 used the narrower list). `cleanup_offtopic_articles.py --dry-run` with the new list: **92** saved articles (IDNTIMES 22 of SYS-34's 29 -- the other 7 are `science`/`health`, withdrawn; JPNN `donwori` 24 + `advetorial` 13; PIKIRANRAKYAT `KlikSeleb` 26; WARTAEKONOMI `english_edition` 6). Also: an article with an empty body now goes to `empty_extraction` *before* the filter -- under the allow-set a bare title would have been waved through and chunked to nothing.
  - **SYS-33:** all 486 TIRTO/TEMPO rows (317 saved + 169 rejected) are the slug placeholder; both sites serve a clean og:title; `repair_placeholder_headlines.py --dry-run` read 486/486 titles, 0 failures. **The equality test alone flagged 4,067 rows**, found when the SYS-36 sampler excluded them: 3,580 genuine title-cased, punctuation-free headlines on MEDIAINDONESIA/JPNN/INEWS/KOMPAS/... coincide with their own slug ("Keadilan Terkubur Longsor"). Requiring the slug's id tail (`-hCa5`/`-hCth` mixed case, `-2287141` digits, not a bare year) gives TIRTO 286 + TEMPO 200 exactly, 20 harmless coincidences elsewhere ("Harga Perak ... Naik Rp 500").
  - **SYS-36:** rubric + prompt in `newscrawler/core/filter_rubric.py` (SYS-37's class list and OPS-18's prompt import it). Sample frozen to `tests/fixtures/filter_gold_sample.jsonl` **before** the re-queue/cleanup (both change the strata): 4,000 of 129,391 rows, 1,019 with body, 800 test / 3,200 train, per stratum: finance_reject 600 (of 1,910), soft_save 92 (all), religion_boundary 200, econ_politics_boundary 400, viva_general 500, mixed_bucket 1,058, finance_saved 400, finance_pending 150, hard_news 400, soft_category 200. Round-robin over (source, category, status) cells inside a stratum so VIVA `blog` (1,000 rows) cannot crowd out `militer`. TIRTO/TEMPO contribute 0 rows by construction (all placeholders until the SYS-33 repair). Smoke test, 2 calls / 40 rows: every `finance_reject` row labelled `is_news=true` with a finance topic, the Timnas headline in a mixed bucket `soft`; **gemini-2.5-flash takes ~30 s per 20-row call with the default (thinking on) config**. **Blocked at 400 rows / 20 calls: this project's Gemini free tier is 20 requests/day/model** (`429 ... GenerateRequestsPerDayPerProjectPerModel-FreeTier, limit: 20`, confirmed daily) -- OPS-20 (#136), owner decision (paid tier, < $1, or ~9 daily runs). The 400 done are all WARTAEKONOMI (file order); the labeller now batches the remainder in a seeded shuffle so a quota-limited day spans every stratum. Early read: `finance_reject` 92.3% is_news, `finance_saved` 98.2%, `soft_save` 0%.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **Sample titles before listing a category name anywhere -- deny-list, allow-set, branch drop.** Of 52 names sampled today, 5 would have been placed wrongly by name (`muslim`, `ototekno`, `edukasi` are news; `donwori`, `KlikSeleb` are soft). (2) **When a fleet probe prints a flag for two sources and one gets a ticket, check the other** -- JPNN's `URL-KEYS` sat in the 09-20 entry. (3) **A detector built from one source's rows must be run over the whole table before it is trusted** -- the placeholder test was right on 486 TIRTO/TEMPO rows and wrong on 3,580 others. (4) **Freeze a labelling sample to a file before running the DB writes that would change its strata**; the re-queue flips the very population SYS-35 was filed on. (5) **`DAILY_API_CALL_LIMIT=1400` is fiction on the free tier: the API enforces 20 requests/day/model on this project**, and OPS-09's (correct) transient handling means the pipeline's only signal is a 429 in a log nobody reads -- budget every Gemini plan against the quota the API states, not the constant. (6) A PowerShell hashtable of single-pair arrays flattens; a `.Replace('s','e')` over two test files followed -- edit with the Edit tool, mutate with a Python snippet, and stage before mutating (`git checkout --` restores the *committed* file, not your edit).
- **SYS-36 completed the same evening on a different labeller (owner decision on OPS-20):** the owner supplied an OpenAI-compatible key (SumoPod, `https://ai.sumopod.com/v1`, model `deepseek-v4-flash`); `label_filter_gold.py` gained `--provider openai` (`OPENAI_API_KEY`/`OPENAI_BASE_URL`/`OPENAI_MODEL` in `constants.py`, `openai==2.48.0` pinned). **`deepseek-v4-flash` is a reasoning model**: with defaults it spent all 4,000 output tokens in `reasoning_content` on a 20-row batch and returned an empty answer (`finish=length`); `reasoning_effort=low` did the same; `extra_body={"thinking": {"type": "disabled"}}` gave 20/20 labels in 420 output tokens and **2.4 s per call**. The 400 Gemini labels were moved aside (`tests/fixtures/filter_gold_gemini400.jsonl`) so the gold set has one labeller; all 4,000 rows labelled in 200 calls with `--workers 4`, 0 rejected, 0 failed, under 3 minutes. Distribution: `soft` 1,319 (33%); per stratum is_news -- hard_news 91.5%, finance_saved 89.8%, econ_politics_boundary 84.0%, finance_reject **79.8%** (the other fifth is corporate press-release material on WARTAEKONOMI's sub-desks, which the rubric files as soft -- and which the SYS-35 allow-set now saves; noted for SYS-37), finance_pending 77.3%, religion_boundary 65.5%, mixed_bucket 59.3%, viva_general 44.6%, soft_save 20.7%, soft_category 12.5%; every topic >= 100 rows except `tech_science` 77. **Inter-labeller agreement on the 400 shared rows: gemini-2.5-flash vs deepseek-v4-flash is_news 94.8%, topic 71.2%** -- the topic disagreements are finance sub-topic boundaries (`corporate` vs `commodities_energy` 12, vs `tech_science` 6, vs `markets` 5, vs `consumer` 5); the is_news ones are PR/advertorial rows Gemini kept and DeepSeek dropped. Hand-check CSV (100 is_news + 48 topic) handed to the owner. **Hand check returned 2026-09-21 (filled in Excel: UTF-8 BOM, `;` delimiter, TRUE/FALSE upper-cased -- `score-handcheck` now sniffs the delimiter and reads utf-8-sig): is_news 96/100 = 96.0% (gate 95), topic 47/48 = 97.9% (gate 85). Gate met; labels accepted. Of the 4 is_news disagreements, 1 is a model error ("Jelang Laga Persib, Ratusan Barang Berbahaya Disita Polda Jabar" -- a police seizure labelled soft on the `Persib` cue), 1 is borderline (a presidential photo-op), and 2 are the owner marking soft what the rubric explicitly calls news (a Kemenag school-phone rule = education policy; Rizky Billar named a KDRT suspect = a celebrity in a court case). Rubric kept as written pending the owner's word. The one topic disagreement is `other_news` vs `consumer` on a halal-feed import-control story.**
- **Executed, same day, after owner approval (15:20-15:35 UTC), in this order and all as predicted by the dry-runs:** `fix_url_shaped_categories.py` 6,786 rows (CNN 682 → 9 names, JPNN 6,104 → 63), 0 URL categories left · `cleanup_offtopic_articles.py` **97** (the 92 + CNN's 5 `gaya-hidup`, visible only once the category was a name), 350 chunks, 97 L1 + 253 L2 vectors · `requeue_finance_rejects.py` **2,105** (the 2,080 + CNN's 25 `ekonomi` rejects, same reason), cooldown cleared · `repair_placeholder_headlines.py` **317** (the 169 rejected rows had just become `pending` and were left to the crawler). Then three allowed `task=full_text` runs as the tickets' Done-when: **LIPUTAN6 `bisnis` 111/111 saved** (the re-queued rows; the new stat line read `bisnis=0/111 (0%)` -- correct: these are exactly the rows the score rejects, which is the SYS-35 loss made visible per run), **TIRTO 95/95 saved with placeholders 95 → 0** (`replaced 10 URL-slug placeholder headline(s)` per batch), IDNTIMES `business` 46/57 (11 fetch failures). IDNTIMES's 62 soft-category backlog rows, pushed through `crawl_newsdetails(specific_sitemaps=…)` because the morning run had put them inside the cooldown: `extracted=54 passed_filter=0 saved=0`, `deny=54`. Final state: 0 URL-shaped categories on CNN/JPNN, 0 saved articles under a denied category, 0 finance-category `rejected_filter` rows, 0 placeholder headlines on fetched rows; `sitemaps` = 92,010 pending / 26,442 rejected_filter / 11,448 saved. **Order matters for the counts**: the rewrite before the cleanup and the re-queue is what let the CNN rows be seen by name; the re-queue before the headline repair is what shrank the repair to 317.

### 2026-09-21 — QA audit of the Phase 0 closures (SYS-22, SRC-11, SRC-36/SRC-41, SYS-35/SYS-34, SYS-33, SYS-36): do they hold, and what did they leave behind?
- **Question / hypothesis:** eight tickets closed on 2026-09-20/21 with four owner-approved production writes and a 4,000-row gold fixture underneath everything in Phase 1. Per the QA rules: verify every Done-when on the live `newsaggregator` DB (not the test DB), mutation-test every fix with mutants the dev did *not* list, dry-run every write script again for idempotence, and read the gold set as data rather than as a row count.
- **Method:** `tests/unit` (657) and `tests/integration` (104) as baseline · a read-only SQL runner against production for each Done-when · `probe_branch_scope.py --website CNN,JPNN` live · all four repair scripts `--dry-run` against production · `check_store_consistency.py` · a gate-rule audit over all 569 live `(source, category)` pairs (which deny/allow rule fires, and why) · a gold-set integrity script (duplicates, split leakage, label validity, `category` vs the live DB by `sitemap_id`, is_news rate per gate) · `score-handcheck` re-run · **29 mutants**, each applied to the CRLF source with `\n`→`\r\n` (12 of 29 silently skipped on the first pass until that was fixed -- the 2026-09-19 trap again), `tests/unit -x` per mutant, file restored, `git status` clean at the end. Scratch scripts (`gold_audit.py`, `gate_audit.py`, `mutate.py`) were throwaway; nothing written to the DB.
- **Result -- Done-whens on the live DB, all hold:** `sitemaps.category LIKE 'http%'` → CNN 0, JPNN 0 (VIVA 20,320 + INEWS 657 remain, SRC-33/SRC-29 as recorded) · finance-category `rejected_filter` rows → 0 · TIRTO 286 saved / 0 placeholder, TEMPO 126 saved + 74 pending, **0 placeholders the id-tail test misses and 0 TIRTO/TEMPO links without an id tail** · saved articles under a `deny` category → 0 · `crawl_log` shows the four verification runs exactly as claimed (LIPUTAN6 111, IDNTIMES 46 + 0, TIRTO 95) · `grep -rn "eval("` empty · SYS-22 live: the 4 SUARA rows since the fix carry whole phrases, 0 comma-glued tokens · all four scripts re-run `--dry-run` → "Nothing to do" · store consistency 9/9 checks at or below baseline after the 97-article cleanup · `probe_branch_scope.py`: CNN 9 branches / JPNN 90, no `URL-KEYS` · `score-handcheck` reproduces 96/100 and 47/48 · `save_sitemaps` never updates an existing row, so a re-listed TIRTO slug cannot clobber a repaired og:title.
- **Result -- one claim inaccurate:** SYS-36's closing comment says `api_usage_log` holds `gemini-2.5-flash` **20**; the row says **202**. The first labeller (commit `11d9c40`) incremented *before* `generate_content`, so the 182 quota-429 retries were counted; `fb45033` moved the increment after the response. Code is right, the 2026-09-20 row overstates Gemini usage by 182 -- a log artefact, not a defect; noted here rather than ticketed.
- **Result -- mutation pass, 23/29 caught in `tests/unit`, 1 more in `tests/integration`:**

- **Result -- gate-rule audit over 569 live pairs (the deny-list as it actually bites):** every substring/word deny is a soft vertical (`bali/sport`, 15× `<region>/olahraga`, `KlikSeleb`, `renungan-ramadan`, `tafsir-al-mishbah`, `food-travel`, `bola-sports`, `inews-tv/religi`, `wisata.viva.co.id`) **except one: INEWS `celebes` (celebes.inews.id, Sulawesi) is denied by the `celeb` stem** -- 200 rows, all `pending`, never attempted, posted 09-18/19, i.e. the newest rows in INEWS's queue; both `celebes` rows in the gold set are labelled news. → **SYS-42 (#137)**.
- **Result -- the gold set as data (`tests/fixtures/filter_gold.jsonl`):** 4,000 rows, 0 duplicate `sitemap_id`, 0 invalid labels, one labeller, 800/3,200 split exactly 20% per stratum, `has_body` consistent, 0 headlines changed in the DB since. But:
  - **218 rows share a headline with another row (61 groups; 214 are INEWS regional mirror hosts -- 319 INEWS rows, 126 "categories", 164 distinct headlines). 46 of 800 test rows have an identical headline in train. 8 groups carry both `is_news` values** (`Moment Hangat Presiden Prabowo … Gontor` ×12 → 7 news / 5 soft) -- the round-robin over `(source, category, status)` cells draws a syndicated article once per host, and temperature 0 does not make the label batch-independent. Two of the owner's four hand-check disagreements are those two headlines. → **SYS-43 (#138)**.
  - **336 rows (JPNN 293, CNN 43) carry a `category` the DB no longer has** (URL-shaped; the sample was frozen 40 min before the SRC-36/41 rewrite). Re-gated on today's category: 178 are `allow` -- 135 of them stratified as `mixed_bucket`, the stratum SYS-37's precision gate is measured on. → **SYS-44 (#139)**.
  - **Allow-set precision: 1,673 gold rows under `gate=allow`, 239 (14.3%) soft** -- ANTARANEWS `Ekonomi` 12/18, SINDONEWS `ekbis` 17/36, IDXCHANNEL `economia` 17/36, `the-founder` 8/18, IDNTIMES `business` 13/36, KUMPARAN `bisnis` 8/25, JPNN `crypto` 11/35 (tips, listicles, CSR/PR reprints, personal-finance how-tos -- the labeller is right on the 40 I read). `finance_reject` stratum (the re-queued population) 20.2% soft vs `finance_saved` (what the old score kept) 10.2% soft: the allow gate bought 30-55% recall with ~6 points of precision, and 6 of the 34 gold `finance_reject` rows re-saved on 09-20 are soft. Deny-set: 19.8% labelled news, mostly labeller noise on features/op-eds it cannot see the category of, plus the `celebes` rows. → **SYS-45 (#140)**, placed with SYS-37.
  - Hand check: 96/100 on `n=100` is a pass at the 95% gate with a wide interval (95% CI ≈ 90–99%); 2 of the 4 disagreements are owner-vs-rubric boundary calls the dev already flagged (school-phone rule; celebrity KDRT suspect). Owner decision still pending; not a ticket.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **Every Phase 0 Done-when holds on the live DB and the regression guards are real** (23/29 + 1) -- the closures stand. (2) The gaps are all *downstream of the writes*, in what the gold set now makes measurable: a stem collision (SYS-42), a fixture built before the metadata it carries was corrected (SYS-44), a sampler that never dedups (SYS-43), and a gate whose precision nobody could measure before 09-20 (SYS-45). **SYS-37 must not train or gate on `filter_gold.jsonl` until SYS-43/SYS-44 are fixed** -- the leak alone inflates its test metrics. (3) Four traps added above. (4) The SYS-34 vocabulary-sampling method was right for the *names it added* and blind to the *names those stems collide with*; the gate-rule audit (5 s, read-only) is the missing second half -- run it after any change to the two keyword lists.
- **Links:** SYS-42 (#137) · SYS-43 (#138) · SYS-44 (#139) · SYS-45 (#140) · DBT-32 (#141) filed · no production writes · `crawler_health/store-consistency-2026-09-21.json`

### 2026-09-21 — Phase 0.5: SYS-42, SYS-43/SYS-44, DBT-32 worked; SYS-45 proposed on SYS-37
- **Question / hypothesis:** the five QA tickets from the Phase 0 audit, in the HANDOFF's order. The open design question was SYS-43/SYS-44's: the HANDOFF said "fix the sampler, `sample` → `label`", but does a re-draw even reproduce the strata the set exists for?
- **Method:** SYS-42 -- `celeb` moved to the word-bounded stem list, whole word `celebrity` onto the substring list, gate-rule audit re-run over all 569 live `(source, category)` pairs, the 452 not-denied pairs frozen to `tests/fixtures/category_vocabulary_not_denied.json` and swept by a test. SYS-43/44 -- a `refresh --dry-run` of the committed sample against the live DB before deciding; then `refresh`. DBT-32 -- the QA mutants re-run after each guard. Mutation harness converts `\n`→`\r\n` for CRLF files (three of the target files are CRLF).
- **Result:**
  - **A re-draw cannot reproduce the 2026-09-20 strata.** `finance_reject` = finance category AND `rejected_filter` AND no body: after SYS-35's re-queue there are **0** such rows in the DB (2,105 → `pending`, 252 → `saved`); `soft_save` = soft category with a saved article: **0** after SYS-34's cleanup. A fresh `sample` today produces a gold set with the SYS-35 recall population missing. The frozen sample is the only record of it, so it was repaired in place (`refresh`) and the sampler fixed for the next deliberately-new set. Documented in the `sample` docstring.
  - **`refresh` on the committed files** (dry-run counts identical to the real run): categories refreshed 336 (JPNN 293, CNN 43) · strata reassigned 141 · duplicate rows dropped 156 (218 rows / 61 groups → one per (source, headline); 1 cross-source wire-copy group kept, on one side) · split rows moved 0 (every one of QA's 46 leaks was an INEWS within-source copy and left with the duplicates) · conflicting-label groups un-labelled 8 → sample 3,844, gold 3,836. INEWS 319 rows / 164 headlines → 164 / 164. Per-stratum test share 18–20% (hard_news gained 130 rows carrying their old split). Six fixture invariants in `TestCommittedGoldFixture`, all red on the old file, green now.
  - **Labeller inter-batch disagreement (SYS-43 Done-when):** of the 61 headlines that appeared more than once in the 2026-09-20 set, **8 (13%) were labelled both `is_news=true` and `false`** by `deepseek-v4-flash` at temperature 0 -- `Moment Hangat Presiden Prabowo … Gontor` ×12 → 7 news / 5 soft. That is the noise floor on *borderline regional features*, not on the set at large (the hand check measured 96%); it is the reason a row that survives dedup out of such a group is re-labelled rather than kept.
  - **SYS-42 live gate audit after the change:** 117 of 569 pairs denied, `celebes` the only change; `celebrity` (OKEZONE) still denied. Mutants: `celeb` back on the substring list → 1 red; gate order swapped → 1 red (the old order test used `bali/sport`, which never matched the allow-set).
  - **DBT-32:** 8/8 re-run mutants red (M08, M18 unit+live, M28, four on `repair_placeholder_headlines.py`, whose write loop is now `apply_titles()` so the live test can drive it without the network). `tests/unit` 657 → 687, `tests/integration` 104 → 106.
  - **SYS-45:** proposed on SYS-37 as an added gate -- allow-set precision ≥ 0.90 on the `test` split with the existing recall gates unchanged -- and the post-probe cleanup of the soft allow-set saves since 2026-09-20 15:20 UTC. Owner decision (stop point 8), nothing shipped.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **Before re-drawing a labelled sample, check that its strata still exist in the DB** -- a stratum defined by a state a later repair script clears is only recoverable from the frozen file (or the repair's backup). (2) `refresh` is the right tool for metadata drift (category rewrites: SRC-33 for VIVA and SRC-29 for INEWS will need it again); a re-draw is for a new population. (3) The next stem on either deny keyword list is checked against the frozen not-denied vocabulary by a test; when a source's vocabulary changes, regenerate the fixture from the DB in the same commit as the deny-list change.

### 2026-09-21 — SYS-46: the rubric moves on two boundaries; what a full re-label of the gold set does to the labels
- **Question / hypothesis:** the owner overruled the rubric on the two hand-check disagreements (school/campus rules → soft; a celebrity's own criminal case → soft). A rubric change re-derives every label. How much of the 4k set actually moves, and how much of the movement is the boundary vs the labeller re-rolling borderline rows?
- **Method:** `filter_rubric.py` amended (worked negatives = the two hand-check rows), `PRD.md` one sentence, prompt pinned by a test. Old labels snapshotted (`811e909`), gold cleared, `label --max-calls 1` smoke, then `label --workers 4`: 193 calls, 3,844 rows, 0 rejected, 0 failed, ~2 min. Diff by `sitemap_id` against the snapshot; boundary attribution by headline keywords (school: `sekolah|siswa|kampus|ujian|seragam|guru|...`; celebrity crime: old `law_crime` + `tersangka|kdrt|narkoba|sidang|...`).
- **Result:**
  - **`is_news` agreement old vs new: 3,636 / 3,836 = 94.8%** — 160 news→soft, 40 soft→news. `soft` 1,261 → 1,388 (33% → 36%).
  - **On the two moved boundaries: ~24 of the 160** (17 school-shaped, 7 celebrity-crime). Both motivating rows are soft now; the whole Rizky Billar/Lesti Kejora group (11 KlikSeleb rows) is soft; `Kemenag Batasi Penggunaan HP di Sekolah` is soft.
  - **The other ~136 news→soft and all 40 soft→news are the labeller re-rolling borderline rows** — the same text under a rubric whose relevant sentences did not change: `Unilever Indonesia Raih HR Asia Awards` news→soft (right), `Vaksin Ebola, antara Harapan dan Tantangan` news→soft (an op-ed; right), `Synology Hadirkan Teknologi Penyimpanan Data Baru` soft→news (product PR; wrong). This is the third measurement of the same number: Gemini-vs-DeepSeek 94.8% on 400 rows (SYS-36), 13% disagreement on repeated borderline headlines (SYS-43), now 94.8% run-to-run on the full set. **The gold set's `is_news` noise floor is ~5%; SYS-37's 0.95 recall gate sits on it.** A recall of 0.95 measured against these labels is indistinguishable from perfect; anything below ~0.93 is a real miss.
  - By old topic, news→soft: health_education 30, corporate 26, other_news 24, law_crime 20, politics_government 19. `health_education` 109 → 74 and `other_news` 123 → 91 — the two classes the boundaries touch shrank most; `tech_science` 77 → 69 (already the weak class). Per stratum `is_news`: mixed_bucket 49.6%, finance_reject 77.7%, hard_news 87.9%, viva_general 42.8%, finance_saved 87.8%, econ_politics 81.8%, soft_category 10.3%, religion 60.1%, finance_pending 76.3%, soft_save 16.1%.
  - Fresh hand check written (100 `is_news` across 10 strata + 48 `topic`), old filled CSV in `e69396e`. The Persib-seizure row (`Jelang Laga Persib, Ratusan Barang Berbahaya Disita Polda Jabar`) is still soft — the model error the owner caught last time, unchanged by the rubric.
- **Conclusion / do not repeat:** (1) **A rubric change costs a full re-label and a full hand check, and moves ~5% of labels that have nothing to do with the change** — batch the boundary decisions, do not re-label per decision. (2) **Quote the labeller's noise floor next to any gate measured on the gold set**: SYS-37 should report recall/precision with a ±0.05 band, and a majority-of-3 label on the `test` split (3 calls per batch, cents) would halve it if the gate ever has to be argued. Filed as a consideration on SYS-37, not done here. (3) The two worked negatives in the prompt did their job (the exact rows flipped, and the group around them); the heuristic attribution is approximate — the hand check is the arbiter.
